金融市場投資週報                 2026 年 10 月 27 日
上週重點訊息
◼ AI 投資回報疑慮升溫，資金轉向債市避險：市場對大型科技企業 AI 基礎建設投資規模、融資
需求及未來獲利回收能力的疑慮升溫，導致科技股賣壓擴大，10 月 22 日 Nasdaq 指數下跌
約 2.6%。隨著市場風險偏好下降，避險資金轉向美國公債，10 年期公債殖利率單日下降約 19
個基點，創 2023 年 3 月以來最大單日跌幅，並於 23 日回升至 4.99%。市場關注焦點逐漸由
AI 投資規模轉向資本支出效益，後續大型科技企業財報及現金流表現，將成為評估 AI 產業評

價合理性的重要依據。
◼ 中東衝突推升通膨風險：中東衝突持續升溫，伊朗支持的葉門胡塞武裝攻擊沙烏地阿拉伯，引
發市場對原油供應中斷的擔憂，推升布蘭特原油價格於 10 月 23 日升至每桶 116.72 美元，單
週上漲約 6.2%，創 2022 年以來最高週收盤水準。能源價格攀升加劇全球通膨壓力，並壓抑
市場風險偏好。
◼ 法國政治動盪加劇財政疑慮，歐洲主權債風險重新受到關注：法國因生活成本上升爆發大規模
抗議，進一步加深市場對政府財政整頓能力的疑慮。10 月 19 日法國 10 年期公債殖利率上升
約 10 個基點至 4.88%附近，與德國公債利差擴大至約 144 個基點，創 2012 年以來最高收盤
水準，法股單日下跌約 1.5%，歐元同步走弱。市場擔憂政治不確定性可能削弱財政改革進度，
並透過主權債評價影響銀行業資產負債表。後續須關注法國財政政策、歐洲主權債利差及銀行
融資成本變化。
市場展望
◼ 科技股短線宜審慎，中期聚焦基本面穩健的 AI 企業：近期 AI 資本支出、融資需求及評價調整

使科技股波動加劇，但先進製程與半導體設備需求仍具韌性，AI 中長期成長趨勢未改變。隨市
場焦點由成長題材轉向投資效益，企業營收與訂單能見度、自由現金流及資本效率的重要性提
升，具技術優勢、財務穩健及獲利能見度的企業相對有利，而高度依賴外部融資者宜審慎看待。
◼ 美債避險功能重現，中天期債券風險報酬相對均衡：市場風險偏好降溫時，美國公債仍具一定
避險效果。儘管 Fed 貨幣政策路徑與能源價格增添利率不確定性，短天期債券易受政策利率
牽動，超長天期債券面臨財政赤字、國債供給及期限溢價等壓力，相較之下，中天期美債的風
險報酬較為均衡。信用債方面，投資等級企業基本面及現金流仍具韌性，後續仍可持續關注。

Document 056: 2026年10月宏观数据点评：生产需求均有回落

生产需求均有回落
——2026 年 10 月宏观数据点评
[Table_Industry]日期: shzqdatemark [Table_Summary] 主要观点
生产需求均有回落
作为经济成效综合体现的工业生产增速有所放缓。三大门类产业增速
均有所下滑。10 月主要行业中食品、医药、电子设备、有色金属冶炼
有所回升；纺织、专用设备、汽车、其他运输设备、黑色金属冶炼有
所下降，其中汽车下降幅度较大。
固定投资增速上月企稳后，跌幅再度扩大。分项来看，制造业投资下
降 2.9%，基础设施投资同比下降 5.1%，跌幅继续扩大。下阶段，要
统筹好各类政府投资基金，加快重大项目开工落地，持续激发民间投
资活力，推动扩大有效投资，促进高质量发展。房地产开发投资降幅
仍在扩大。分项来看，地产销售面积跌幅扩大；供给端施工面积跌幅
扩大，新开工面积跌幅小幅收窄。从房企开发资金的来源来看，房地
产资金跌幅有所扩大，分项中除个人按揭项外跌幅仍在扩大。地产投
资仍待企稳。

10 月份，社会消费品零售总额 46463 亿元，同比增长 0.4%。具体来
看，各品类消费除家具外均有所下降，通讯器材跌幅较大汽车消费仍
偏低迷。10 月商品零售、餐饮消费增速均有所下滑。
加力扩大内需，优化供给
二季度以来，需求放缓，投资持续负增，消费表现偏软。二季度经济
增速较一季度有所回落，但三季度反弹至 4.5%。前三季度 4.6%的经
济增速也为完成全年目标打下了较好的基础。10 月生产需求均有回
落。政策方面，政治局会议提出实施好更加积极的财政政策和适度宽
松的货币政策，充分发挥各项存量政策效能，及时谋划出台务实管用
的增量政策，加大逆周期调节力度。针对需求方面，会议指出要有效
扩大国内需求，适应不同群体消费需求扩大优质供给，挖掘服务消费
潜力；扎实推进“六张网”规划建设。我们认为在政策助力下，加力
扩大内需、优化供给，推动经济持续向新向优向好发展，努力实现
“十五五”良好开局。
 风险提示
地缘政治事件恶化，国际金融形势改变，中美政策超预期变化。
[Table_Author]分析师: 陈彦利

1 事件：10 月份宏观数据公布
工业生产放缓。10 月份，规模以上工业增加值同比实际增长
4.8%（增加值增速均为扣除价格因素的实际增长率）。从环比看，
10 月份，规模以上工业增加值比上月增长 0.21%。1—10 月份，规
模以上工业增加值同比增长 5.3%。
固定资产跌幅扩大。1—10 月份，全国固定资产投资（不含农
户）364824 亿元，同比下降 8.1%。其中，知识产权产品投资同比
增长 9.2%
消费再度回落。1—10 月份，社会消费品零售总额 416464 亿
元，同比增长 1.0%。其中，除汽车以外的消费品零售额 383811 亿
元，增长 2.8%。10 月份，社会消费品零售总额 46463 亿元，同比
增长 0.4%。其中，除汽车以外的消费品零售额 43162 亿元，增长
2.5%。
表 1：本期经济数据增速（%）
指标 本期值 前值
工业增加值（当月） 4.8 5.5
固定资产投资（不含农户，累计） -8.1 -7.2
社会消费品零售（当月） 0.4 1.1

资料来源：统计局，IFIND，上海证券研究所
2 事件解析：数据特征和变动原因
2.1 工业生产有所放缓
10 月工业生产有所放缓，同比增速下降 0.7 个百分点。分三
大门类看，10 月份，采矿业增加值同比增长 0.7%，制造业增长
5.4%，电力、热力、燃气及水生产和供应业增长 4.4%。三大门类
产业增速均有所下滑。从产量的增速来看，原煤、原油产量小幅
上升；制造业方面，焦炭、水泥、工业机器人、集成电路产量有
所回升；公共事业方面，发电量有所下降、天然气产量有所回升。
另外出口交货值有所回升。

2.2 主要行业生产有升有降
分行业看，与 9 月相比，10 月主要行业中食品、医药、电子
设备、有色金属冶炼有所回升；纺织、专用设备、汽车、其他运
输设备、黑色金属冶炼有所下降，其中汽车下降幅度较大。

资料来源：IFIND，上海证券研究所
2.3 固定投资跌幅持续加深
1—10 月份，全国固定资产投资（不含农户）364824 亿元，

同比下降 8.1%，跌幅进一步加深。其中，第一产业投资 7018 亿
元，同比下降 6.1%；第二产业投资 142333 亿元，下降 3.6%；第
三产业投资 215473 亿元，下降 10.9%。三大产业累计投资增速全
面回落。
固定投资增速上月企稳后，跌幅再度扩大。分项来看，制造
业投资下降 2.9%，基础设施投资同比下降 5.1%，跌幅继续扩大。
下阶段，要统筹好各类政府投资基金，加快重大项目开工落地，
持续激发民间投资活力，推动扩大有效投资，促进高质量发展。

资料来源：IFIND，上海证券研究所
2.4 地产投资仍是拖累
1—10 月份，全国房地产开发投资 57215 亿元，同比下降
21.6%，降幅仍在扩大。分项来看，地产销售面积跌幅扩大；供给
端施工面积跌幅扩大，新开工面积跌幅小幅收窄。从房企开发资
金的来源来看，房地产资金跌幅有所扩大，分项中除个人按揭项
外跌幅仍在扩大。地产投资仍待企稳。

2.5 消费小幅回落
10 月份，社会消费品零售总额 46463 亿元，同比增长 0.4%。
按经营单位所在地分，城镇消费品零售额同比增长 0.2%；乡村消
费品零售额增长 1.5%。城乡消费双双回落。

3 事件影响：对经济和市场
3.1 生产需求均有回落
作为经济成效综合体现的工业生产增速有所放缓。三大门类
产业增速均有所下滑。10 月主要行业中食品、医药、电子设备、
有色金属冶炼有所回升；纺织、专用设备、汽车、其他运输设备、
黑色金属冶炼有所下降，其中汽车下降幅度较大。
固定投资增速上月企稳后，跌幅再度扩大。分项来看，制造
业投资下降 2.9%，基础设施投资同比下降 5.1%，跌幅继续扩大。
下阶段，要统筹好各类政府投资基金，加快重大项目开工落地，
持续激发民间投资活力，推动扩大有效投资，促进高质量发展。
房地产开发投资降幅仍在扩大。分项来看，地产销售面积跌幅扩
大；供给端施工面积跌幅扩大，新开工面积跌幅小幅收窄。从房
企开发资金的来源来看，房地产资金跌幅有所扩大，分项中除个
人按揭项外跌幅仍在扩大。地产投资仍待企稳。
10 月份，社会消费品零售总额 46463 亿元，同比增长 0.4%。

具体来看，各品类消费除家具外均有所下降，通讯器材跌幅较大
汽车消费仍偏低迷。10 月商品零售、餐饮消费增速均有所下滑。
4 事件预测：趋势判断
4.1 加力扩大内需，优化供给

### Page 8

宏观数据
请务必阅读尾页重要声明 8
二季度以来，需求放缓，投资持续负增，消费表现偏软。二
季度经济增速较一季度有所回落，但三季度反弹至 4.5%。前三季
度 4.6%的经济增速也为完成全年目标打下了较好的基础。10 月生
产需求均有回落。政策方面，政治局会议提出实施好更加积极的
财政政策和适度宽松的货币政策，充分发挥各项存量政策效能，
及时谋划出台务实管用的增量政策，加大逆周期调节力度。针对
需求方面，会议指出要有效扩大国内需求，适应不同群体消费需
求扩大优质供给，挖掘服务消费潜力；扎实推进“六张网”规划
建设。我们认为在政策助力下，加力扩大内需、优化供给，推动
经济持续向新向优向好发展，努力实现“十五五”良好开局。

地缘政治事件恶化，国际金融形势改变，中美政策超预期变
化。

投资评级体系与评级定义
股票投资评级： 分析师给出下列评级中的其中一项代表其根据公司基本面及（或）估值预期以报告日起 6 个月
内公司股价相对于同期市场基准指数表现的看法。
买入 股价表现将强于基准指数 20%以上
增持 股价表现将强于基准指数 5-20%
中性 股价表现将介于基准指数±5%之间
减持 股价表现将弱于基准指数 5%以上
无评级 由于我们无法获取必要的资料，或者公司面临无法预见结果的重大不确定性事
件，或者其他原因，致使我们无法给出明确的投资评级

2026 年 11 月 17 日

調査レポート

2026 年 7～9 月期の GDP（1 次速報）結果

～個人消費が力強さを欠き、4 四半期ぶりのマイナス成長～

調査部 主任研究員 藤田隼平

2026 年 11 月 16 日に内閣府が公表した 7～9 月期の実質 GDP（1 次速報値）は、前期比－0.0%（年率換算   
－0.1%）と減少した。設備投資が前期比＋1.6%と高めの伸びとなった一方、個人消費が同－0.3%と 2 四半期連
続で減少したことなどが下押し要因となり、小幅ながら 4 四半期ぶりのマイナス成長となった。なお、輸入が鉱物
性燃料を中心に持ち直したことで外需寄与度は－0.6%ポイントと大きめのマイナスとなったが、公的在庫の寄与
度が＋0.5%ポイントとなり、おおむね相殺された。 
過去 3 四半期にわたって潜在成長率を上回る高めの伸びが続いていたことや、設備投資が堅調に増加したこ
とを踏まえると、今回のマイナス成長をもって景気回復の動きが途切れたとは判断できないが、景気が力強さを欠
いていることも事実だろう。特に GDP の過半を占める個人消費に弱さがみられたことは懸念材料である。今後は、
良好な雇用・所得環境に支えられて個人消費は持ち直しへ向かうと期待されるが、消費者物価の伸び拡大や住
宅ローン金利の上昇による家計負担の増加を通じて、消費の減少が続くリスクには警戒が必要である。 
なお、物価上昇分を含んだ名目 GDP は、前期比＋0.4%（年率換算＋1.6%）と、10 四半期連続のプラス成長
となった。物価上昇圧力は依然として強く、コスト増加分の価格転嫁が進む中で、GDP デフレーターは前年比  
＋2.4%（季節調整済前期比では＋0.4%）と、高い伸びが続いた。

（注）内需寄与度、民間在庫、外需寄与度は実質 GDP に対する寄与度（%ポイント）

 1 次速報値を踏まえた、2026 年 7～9 月期における需要項目別の動向（実質ベース）は以下の通り。

① 実質個人消費（前期比－0.3%） 
 家計を取り巻く所得環境は改善した（実質雇用者報酬は前期比＋0.3%）が、前期に自動車や
エアコンが一時的な要因によって増加した反動で耐久財が前期比－3.5%と減少した上、夏場
の地震や台風、長雨などの自然災害・天候不順によって消費の機会に恵まれなかったこと、食
料品の値上げや住宅ローン金利の上昇など今後の生活不安につながる話題も多く節約志向が
強まったことから、半耐久財や非耐久財も減少した。ただし、サービスは 9 月のシルバーウイー
クの日並びが良かったこともあり、小幅に増加した。 
② 実質住宅投資（前期比－1.1%） 
 住宅投資に先行する住宅着工戸数が 2 四半期ぶりに減少する中、建築コストの増加も逆風とな
り、住宅投資は 2 四半期連続で減少した。 
③ 実質設備投資（前期比＋1.6%） 
 企業の省力化や生産性向上に向けた投資意欲は強く、特に 7～9 月期はサーバーや半導体製
造装置といった AI 関連投資も好調だったことで、設備投資は 3 四半期ぶりに増加した。 
④ 実質民間在庫（前期比寄与度＋0.0%ポイント） 
 前期に大きめのプラス寄与となったこともあり、民間在庫の寄与は横ばいとなった。 
 なお、公的在庫は国家備蓄原油の減少幅が縮小したことから、前期比寄与度＋0.5%ポイントと
なった。 
⑤ 実質政府最終消費（前期比－0.0%） 
 前期に高めの伸びとなったこともあり、横ばいだった。 
⑥ 実質公共投資（前期比－1.1%） 
 名目では増加したものの、工事コストの増加を受けたデフレーターの上昇に追いついておらず、
実質では 2 四半期連続で減少した。 
⑦ 外需（前期比寄与度－0.6%ポイント、実質輸出：前期比－0.2%、実質輸入：同＋2.5%） 
 輸出は前期比－0.2%と減少した。財は堅調な米国向けや中東向けの持ち直しを受けて増加し
た一方、サービスは前期に研究開発サービスが一時的な要因により上振れた反動で減少した。 
 輸入は前期比＋2.5%と増加した。特に財が鉱物性燃料を中心に持ち直した。ただし、サービス
については減少した。 
 結果、輸出から輸入を差し引いた外需の寄与度は－0.6%ポイントと大きめのマイナスになった。

Despite strong 3Q26 results and resilient near-term guidance at
both WT Microelectronics and WPG Holdings, we see visibility
risks into 2028. Memory price normalization and rising USD
funding costs prompt us to trim expectations across Greater
China semi distributors.
Peaking momentum meets rising 2027-28 headwinds: Both WT and WPG posted

record top-lines and profit scale in 3Q26 on AI server buildouts and sharp memory
price inflation; yet, we are wary of several late-cycle pressures. First, on AI
infrastructure, our US team recently shifted ~5 GW of US data center capacity from
2028 into 2029 post-US midterms (link), introducing procurement timing risk.
Second, we caution that memory may still be cyclical after all, and we may now be
late into the cycle; (Old Memory: Peak Pricing Power?; Memory – Key Debate Post

Mid-terms (Shawn Kim)).  Third, elevated memory prices are already weighing on
traditional consumer end markets. Finally, rapid revenue expansion has driven heavy
working capital outflows and debt accumulation just as our US Economics team
forecasts two 25 bps Fed hikes in Dec and Mar to 4.25%-4.50%, keeping USD
financing costs elevated (link).
WT Microelectronics (3036.TW) 3Q26 review (link): For 4Q26, management
guided revenue to reaccelerate to a midpoint of NT$750bn (+26% Q/Q, +119% Y/Y)

on high double-digit Q/Q data center growth, with GM stepping down to a midpoint
of 3.24% (-0.36ppt Q/Q) on mix. Net profit guidance was a midpoint of NT$11.5bn
(+12% Q/Q, +17% Y/Y). 4Q26 financing costs were guided up to NT$2.7bn (vs. NT
$1.94bn in 3Q26) with no timeline for operating cash flow turning positive.
WPG Holdings (3702.TW) 3Q26 review: 3Q26 revenue was NT$510bn (+11.8% Q/Q,
+108.6% Y/Y), GM was 4.42%, and net profit was NT$10.39bn (+20.5% Q/Q,

+226.9% Y/Y). Memory surged to 58% of 3Q26 revenue (from ~25% historically)
and compute (AI and server) reached 52%, while communication/mobile mix was 11%
and consumer mix was 9%. Management noted its ship-and-debit model mitigates
inventory write-down risk, and its LaaS platform grew GMV 75.6% Y/Y (revenue
+15.5% Y/Y). For 4Q26, WPG guided revenue at a midpoint of NT$505bn, -1.0% Q/Q,
GM at a midpoint of 4.40%, and EPS at a midpoint of NT$5.47. Financial cost/sales

as at ~0.8% (two-thirds USD-denominated).
Stock implications: WT: downgrading to EW, PT cut to NT$174. 2026/27/28 EPS
revised by +1%/-6%/-5%. WPG: downgrading to EW, PT cut to NT$79. 2026/27/28
EPS revised by +46%/+10%/-25%.

4
WT Micro: Valuation Methodology
We cut our price target to NT$174: We update our residual income model to reflect
earnings estimate revisions. We reduce our terminal growth rate assumption from 3%  to
2%, in line with longer term global macro economic growth rate. We cut our intermediate
growth rate assumption from 8.0% to 4.0% to reflect demand uncertainties in the end
market. We also raise our beta estimate from 0.90 to 1.10 to reflect potentially peaking

growth and demand uncertainties, and this leads to an increased cost of equity from 13.3%
to 15.6%. Other key RI assumptions are unchanged: 2.0% risk-free rate, and  cash dividend
payout ratio of 85%. We believe WT deserves a high beta due to the high liabilities to
equity ratio (5.92x as of 3Q26) and the strong correlation with the wider Taiwan stock
market due to the nature of the distribution business.
We downgrade WT to EW. We believe the risk factors we’re seeing are likely to persist

and the stock price will likely remain under pressure unless there is further clarity or
policy reversals; we do not recommend accumulation even though the stock price has
corrected significantly from its peak in May.

WPG: Estimate Revisions Summary
We revise 2026-28e EPS by +46%/+10%/-25% each:   This factors in 2-3Q26 actual results
and 4Q26 guidance, which led to stronger 2026 estimates, as well as some near-term
resilience into 2027 with sustained memory shipment volume. Further into 2027 and
2028, we factor in an increasingly uncertain investment environment and therefore reduce
our estimates for non-operating income, mainly investment income.

Idea
8
WPG: Valuation Methodology
We cut our price target to NT$79: We update our residual income model to reflect
earnings estimate revisions. We also raise our beta estimate from 1.3 to 1.5 to reflect
potentially peaking growth and demand uncertainties, and raised our risk premium from
6% to 8% to reflect market volatilities; this leads to an increased cost of equity from 9.5%
to 13.9%. We also raise our risk free rate from 1.5% to 2.0% to reflect rising funding costs.

Our intermediate growth rate is reduced from 5.5% to 4.0% to reflect demand
uncertainties. Other key RI assumptions are unchanged: cash dividend payout ratio of
80%, and long-term growth rate of 2.0%. We believe WPG deserves a high beta due to its
high liabilities to equity ratio (5.85x as of 3Q26) and the strong correlation to the wider
Taiwan stock market due to the nature of the distribution business.
We downgrade WPG to EW. We believe the risk factors we’re seeing are likely to persist

and the stock price will likely remain under pressure unless there is further clarity or
policy reversals; we do not recommend accumulation even though the stock price has
corrected significantly from its peak in August.

Risk Reward - WPG Holdings (3702.TW)
NT$79.00
Base case, residual income model. We assume a cost of equity of 13.9% (2.0% risk-free rate,
8% risk premium, 1.5 beta), a medium-term growth rate of 4.0% and a long-term growth rate
of 2.0%.
MS Rating
▪ WPG still has high revenue exposure to
consumer markets, which are likely to
remain weak given high memory costs.
However, the AI story is now strong enough
to lift earnings for WPG.

▪ We are wary of several late-cycle
pressures including potential data center
buildout delay, late memory cycle, weak end
market demand, and rates.
▪ WPG has limited exposure to AI GPUs
(most of which go through direct sales) and
AI ASICs, but has good exposure to AI
computing
▪ Our PT implies 5.8x 2027e EPS

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

### Page 1

M
Update
Lotes Co. Ltd.  | Asia Pacific
Post-AP Summit Taiwan
Corporate Day Feedback
Key takeaways from Lotes:
• Revenue mix ~85% China / ~15% Vietnam. Vietnam is a full function factory,
with Phase I (3 buildings) all under production now, and with more land to
expand for Phase II. Management sees a path for Vietnam to rise to ~20% of
revenue contribution.
• Server is the main growth contributor next year with ~60% y/y sales growth,
driven by a combination of volume (10-15%), ASP (20%+), and new products

(QD, SOCAMM, NPO, etc).
• Management expects 2027 revenue growth to accelerate vs 2026 (1-3Q26
revenue is up 17.7% y/y), driven mainly by new CPU socket adoption increase,
and new products ramp.
• Lotes expects QD revenue to rise to ~10% of revenue contribution in CY27,
from 3-5% currently. Current supply share is still small.
• Lotes is one of the first suppliers for SOCAMM connectors, and management
expects revenue to continue to grow in CY27, from 2-3% of revenue.

• As per management, NPO socket should start to contribute to revenue in
2H27, with more material growth expected in CY28, with adoption across
multiple models.
• Management noted PC revenue declined to 22% of total revenue as of 3Q26
from 36% in CY25, suggesting that further declines in the PC market in CY27
could have a limited impact to Lotes.

Lotes Co. Ltd. (3533.TW)
Primary - Residual Income
Base case, residual income model. Key assumptions include a cost of equity of 9.6% (beta of
1.0, equity risk premium of 8.7% and risk-free rate of 1.0%), a medium-term growth rate of
12.5%, and a terminal growth rate of 3%.
Risks to Upside
n Stronger-than-expected growth in the server business, such as from cloud and enter-
prise in China

n Stronger-than-expected DT demand
n Sooner-than-expected inventory digestion of NB
n Faster ramp-up of its EV business
Risks to Downside
n Further deceleration of server demand from US hyperscalers
n Weaker-than-expected DT demand
n Slower-than-expected inventory digestion of NB
n More severe pricing erosion driven by competition

ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

報告日期：2026.11.16 研究員：陳泂暉

基本資料 結論與建議 
2026/11/13 收盤價： 1,520.00 毛利重返成長軌道，上調投資評等至「買進」 
目前股價 12X27(F)PER，考量： 
(1) 26Q3 公 司 營 收 為 278.54 億元，QoQ+19.6% 、
YoY+51.3%，受惠 HPC 電源需求、SPE 沉積蝕刻及次
系統提升與電氣化推進；毛利率為 32.6% ，
QoQ+1.9ppts、YoY-0.7ppts，費用率創低帶動營益率創
高；稅後淨利 42.78億元，QoQ+43.5%、YoY+62.8%；
稅後 EPS 21.93元。 
(2) 工業業務受惠工廠自動化回溫及能源、電動車等電氣化
需求推進，機器人、航太與醫療等客製化方案亦逐步貢
獻。半導體設備受惠沉積與蝕刻滲透率提升，客戶投資
看至 2030年後，帶動子系統整合與機箱組裝委外需求擴
大。 
(3) HPC受惠 AI基礎設施升級，機櫃內銅纜與機櫃外光纖將
長期共存。公司透過 XFS 強化光學封裝與量產，推進
CPO、12.8T XPO 及 Micro-VCSEL 技術；電源延伸至
Busbars、匯流排槽及水冷板等全液冷機櫃方案，結合
Interplex 機構整合並共同開發 LVDC/HVDC 架構，推升
系統級競爭力。 
(4) 預估 2027 年營收 1,636.24 億元，YoY+54.4%，受惠於
新世代平台放量；毛利率 33.6%，YoY+2.1ppts，受惠於
產品組合改善；稅後淨利 256.69 億元，YoY+74.6%；稅
後 EPS 131.58元。

貿聯目前股價位於 12X27(F)PER，位於公司過往 9-34X區間
下緣位置，考量公司持續推進光通業務，將有助於評價回升，
所以取 17X，給予「買進」投資評等， 6 個月目標價
2,200(17X2027(F)PER)，潛在上漲空間 44.74%。

訪談重點

全球連接線束解決方案領導者 
貿聯成立於 1996 年，總部位於加州矽谷，註冊於開曼群島，為全球連接線
束解決方案的領導廠商。產品分為工業用、電器用、車用、資訊科技與其他
五大產線。主要應用於資訊科技用線、電腦週邊設備、數據中心用線、消費
電子用線、電器用線、車用線、醫療設備用線、光通訊設備、太陽能連接裝
置、工業設備用線、半導體設備等線材、連接器等領域。公司 26Q1 產品營
收比重為: 工業用 31%、電器用 12%、車用 8%、資訊科技 48%。

HPC 與半導體設備雙引擎驅動營益率創歷史新高 
26Q3 公司營收為 278.54 億元，QoQ+19.6%、YoY+51.3%，細分產品組合
來看，HPC 高效能運算受惠於 AI 基礎設施持續投資，其中電源互連因高電
流與機櫃級配電需求強勁、旗下 XFS光學業務月營收運行率自年初以來已超
過翻倍、SPE半導體生產設備受惠於沉積與蝕刻等設備平台滲透率及次系統
承接比重提升，並持續大幅優於整體 WFE 市場、其他業務整體市場環境好
壞參半，但電氣化與自動化需求持續推進，另併購之 Interplex於 26Q3 僅計
入 7天營收，10月起完整併表並單月貢獻 6,700萬美元；毛利率為 32.6%，
QoQ+1.9ppts、YoY-0.7ppts，搭配營業費用率降至歷史新低，帶動營業利益
率創下歷史新高；稅後淨利 42.78 億元，QoQ+43.5%、YoY+62.8%；稅後
EPS 21.93元。

動能從單一零組件邁向子系統級解決方案，動能延續至 2027年後 
工業用業務方面，受惠工廠自動化需求活動持續改善，加上受惠於電氣化與
基礎設施現代化的能源、電動車及特用工程應用穩健推進，帶動整體表現持
續成長；此外，公司在機器人、航太、海事、醫療科技、半導體量測及儲能
等領域的客製化線束與整合方案，部分已成為穩定貢獻來源，部分則處於早
期認證與商業開發階段。

半導體生產設備方面，主要受惠於沉積與蝕刻設備平台滲透率與內含價值提
升，且客戶針對高複雜度製程的投資預期強勁並延伸至 2030 年以後，帶動
高複雜度組裝、子系統製造與整合、測試及機箱組裝的委外需求持續擴大。
隨著各產業客戶對供應商的要求從單一零組件轉向具備工程支援、產品認

- 3 - 
訪談速報 
證、量產準備度與穩定執行力的子系統級解決方案，結合併購 Interplex所帶
來的精密機構與機殼整合綜效，相關成長動能預期將延續至 2027 年及更長
遠的基礎設施與產能擴張週期。

機櫃內銅纜、機櫃外光纖並進，提供完整互連方案 
公司持續受惠於高速銅互連解決方案在支援更高頻寬與維持訊號完整性上的
優勢；隨著 AI系統頻寬需求、光纖密度與互連複雜度提高，光互連基礎設施
的重要性持續提升，同時銅互連在機櫃內短距離傳輸、成本與部署特性上仍
具效率優勢，因此光互連不會單向取代銅互連，而是機櫃內以銅纜為主、機
櫃外長距離傳輸以光纖為主，兩者將依頻寬、傳輸距離、功耗與封裝架構不
同而長期共存。公司透過旗下 XFS Communications 補強精密光學組裝、連
接器化製程、高密度封裝與快速量產擴張能力，並陸續於 OFC、Computex
與 OCP展出光纖方案、CPO交換器托盤及 12.8T XPO收發方案，同時推進
次世代基於 Micro-VCSEL的機櫃內擴展（Scale-up）光學互連技術與外部技
術合作，成為後續 HPC業務擴張的重要動能。

在 HPC端，AI基礎設施進入長期投資與升級週期，隨著算力密度持續攀升，
資料中心的物理基礎設施挑戰正延伸至高電流電力傳輸、散熱及系統級整
合。公司產品線已從單一電源線延伸至 Busbars、Power Whips、連接器、
匯流排槽以及水冷板、分歧管、液冷快接頭與漏液偵測等全液冷 AI 機櫃方
案，並結合併購 Interplex的精密機構與機殼整合能力強化系統級競爭力；至
於低壓直流與高壓直流新電源架構的導入管理層指出將取決於各家客戶的系
統設計、產品認證進度及資料中心電力基礎設施的就緒程度，目前公司正與
客戶密切共同開發次世代與下下世代架構，以高度彈性配合其部署排程。

新世代平台放量帶動獲利爆發 
預估 26Q4營收 339.62億元，QoQ+21.9%、YoY+71.7%，因為工業用季節
性需求回溫，以及 HPC 新平台所帶動；毛利率 32.8%，QoQ+0.2ppts、
YoY+0.9ppts，因新平台導入，因此壓抑毛利率成長幅度；稅後淨利 51.7億
元，QoQ+20.9%、YoY+88.7%；稅後 EPS 26.5元。

預估 2026年營收 1,059.61億元，YoY+48.7%，持續受惠於資料中心業務；
毛利率 31.5%，YoY-0.2ppts；稅後淨利 147.04 億元，YoY+63.3%；稅後
EPS 75.37元。

預估 2027年營收 1,636.24 億元，YoY+54.4%，受惠於新世代平台放量；毛
利率 33.6%，YoY+2.1ppts，受惠於產品組合改善；稅後淨利 256.69 億元，
YoY+74.6%；稅後 EPS 131.58元。

貿聯目前股價位於 12X27(F)PER，位於公司過往 9-34X區間下緣位置，考

操作（預期未來六個月股價漲跌幅介於-15%與+15%之間）； 逢高賣出（預期未來六個月股價跌幅介於-15%與-30%之間）；賣出
（預期未來六個月股價跌幅超過-30%）。

鈞興-KY(4571) 
買進, TP 190 (潛在報酬 27.52%)  
買進 
Price(11/25)：149 
Target Price：190

受惠電動工具與智能傳動需求暢旺，鈞興 26Q3 單季 EPS 創歷史
次高，達 2.98 元。營收創歷史新高 9.38 億元，QoQ 28.94%、YoY 
16.27%，毛利率 33.48%，季減 0.76 個百分點、年減 0.74 個百分
點，營業利益 1.86 億元，QoQ 67.18%、YoY 16.80%，稅後淨利
1.56 億元，QoQ 70.41%、YoY 17.18%。 
展望後市，電動工具新案出貨順暢，訂單能見度已達 2027 年 1 月，
預期 26Q4 營收 8.55 億元，QoQ -8.82%、YoY 17.07%，營業利
益 1.70 億元，QoQ -8.47%、YoY 25.64%，EPS 2.68 元，帶動全
年營收創高。 
2027 年電動工具受惠美國基建、製造業回流與客戶新品放量，預
估維持個位數年增，園林工具則視美國利率與房市動向而定。高毛
利智能傳動工業機器人齒輪訂單能見度 3 至 6 個月，2026 年佔比
站穩 10%以上，2027 年營收延續雙位數成長並優化產品組合；人
形機器人已向中台美客戶送樣，預期 2027 年部分客戶進入小量量
產。產能方面，越南二期廠房預計 2026 年底完工、2027 年投產，
強化非中供應鏈布局。 
研究部預估 26/27 年營收 33.05/35.24 億元，YoY 7.83%/6.63%，
毛利率 33.92%/34.11%，年增 1.15/0.19 個百分點，營業利益
6.16/7.32 億元，YoY 15.76%/18.89%，稅後淨利 5.14/5.97 億元，
YoY 3.16%/16.19%，EPS 為 9.82 元/11.26 元。 
評價方面，公司過往 5 年 PER 評價集中於 15x-20x，考量後市客
戶新品上市將帶動營收成長，且產品組合優化能持續挹注獲利表
現，故給予歷史本益比區間中緣偏上之 17x PER 評價，目標價 
190 元，投資建議為買進。  
⚫   ⚫ 公司基本資料 
鈞興主要銷售客製化的全鋼齒輪、粉末治金齒輪、齒輪箱、精
密五金件及諧波減速器等予客戶，生產基地位於廣東珠海廠及
越南廠，產品廣泛用於電動工具、園林工具、工業縫紉機、及
工業機器人等領域，

⚫ 營收獲利穩步成長，估 26/27 年 EPS 9.82 元/11.26 元 
電動工具與智能傳動需求展望正向，越南二期 2027 年投產 
展望後市，受惠電動工具新案出貨順暢，訂單能見度已達 2027
年 1 月，預期 26Q4 將維持穩健 YoY 成長，研究部預期有望
帶動 2026 全年營收創新高。 
進入 2027 年，電動工具受惠美國基建與製造業回流，加上客
戶新品帶動相關齒輪出貨放量，雖歐洲市場仍視戰事進展而
定，研究部預估整體電動工具仍可望維持個位數年增率；園林
工具受 2025 年關稅前拉貨墊高基期影響，後續須觀察美國利
率與房市動向；高毛利之智能傳動業務方面，目前工業機器人
齒輪訂單能見度約 3–6 個月，2026 全年營收佔比可望站穩
10%以上，隨工業自動化需求持續，2027 年營收將延續雙位
數成長，並透過產品組合優化進一步挹注毛利表現。

產能方面，越南廠目前佔生產比重約三成，二期廠房預計 2026
年底完工、2027 年投產，強化非中供應鏈布局。

人形機器人已向中台美廠商送樣 
智能傳動與機器人業務，除穩定供應工業焊接、搬運機械手臂
精密齒輪外，亦積極布局諧波、少齒差減速器與微型齒輪。人
形機器人已向中國、台灣及美國客戶送樣，預期 2027 年部分
客戶進入小量量產，中長期則取決於客戶具體生產規劃以及市
場需求。

綜上所述，主營業務電動工具隨客戶新品放量與終端需求延
續，持續帶動營收成長，預估 26/27 年營收為 33.05 億元/35.24
億元，YoY 7.83%/6.63%；獲利方面，受惠智能傳動營收規模
與佔比提升，預估毛利率 33.92%/34.11%，年增 1.15/0.19 個
百分點；營業利益率 18.63%/20.77%，年增 1.28/2.14 個百分
點；營業利益 6.16 億元/7.32 億元，YoY 15.76%/18.89%；
稅後獲利 5.14 億元/5.97 億元，YoY 3.16%/16.19%；EPS 為
9.82 元/11.26 元。

⚫ 結論與建議: 
受惠電動工具與智能傳動需求暢旺，鈞興 26Q3 單季 EPS 創
歷史次高，達 2.98 元。營收創歷史新高 9.38 億元，QoQ 
28.94%、YoY 16.27%，毛利率 33.48%，季減 0.76 個百分點、
年減 0.74 個百分點，營業利益 1.86 億元，QoQ 67.18%、YoY 
16.80%，稅後淨利 1.56 億元，QoQ 70.41%、YoY 17.18%。 
展望後市，電動工具新案出貨順暢，訂單能見度已達 2027 年
1 月，預期 26Q4 營收 8.55 億元，QoQ -8.82%、YoY 17.07%，
營業利益 1.70 億元，QoQ -8.47%、YoY 25.64%，EPS 2.68
元，帶動全年營收創高。2027 年電動工具受惠美國基建、製
造業回流與客戶新品放量，預估維持個位數年增，園林工具則
視美國利率與房市動向而定。高毛利智能傳動工業機器人齒輪
訂單能見度 3 至 6 個月，2026 年佔比站穩 10%以上，2027
年營收延續雙位數成長並優化產品組合；人形機器人已向中台
美客戶送樣，預期 2027 年部分客戶進入小量量產。產能方面，
越南二期廠房預計 2026 年底完工、2027 年投產，強化非中
供應鏈布局。

股價相對大盤走勢

上品(4770 TT) Trading Buy

先進製程電子特化儲運設備核心受惠股。 
投資建議：半導體應用佔比逐季擴張至 95.10%，晶圓廠擴產帶
動營收增長；氟素樹脂改性與 TEFPASS® 專利內襯工藝，並延
伸至晶圓廠廢酸回收純化系統；目前營收主要來自台灣、中國，
美國市場建廠認列暫入空窗期。預估 2027 稅後 EPS15.34 元，
投資建議 Trading Buy，目標價 296 元。 
3Q26 成長來自半導體產業需求，已成絕對核心成長引擎： 
3Q26 營收 15.98 億元，受惠於半導體晶圓廠建廠與擴產需求強
勁，毛利率 34.64% (QoQ-0.98 ppt)，主因第三季認列部分 2025 
年承接之較低毛利訂單，微幅稀釋單季毛利表現。受惠於營收
規模大幅擴張，單季營業費用率顯著收斂至 9.63%，業外淨收
益  0.16 億元(QoQ-11.71%），主要歸因於利息收入增加及外匯
匯兌變動，稅後淨利 3.41 億元，稅後 EPS4.27 元。 
增長來自台灣、中國市場，美國進入認列空窗期： 
中國與台灣兩大市場合計貢獻 3Q26 營收 95.60%。其中中國
市場受惠於成熟製程晶圓廠建置與電子特化在地化需求，營收
佔比躍升至 48.20%，成為單季最大營收來源區域；台灣市場維
持  47.40% 的高檔佔比，反映本土先進製程晶圓大廠擴產動能
穩固。美國市場因亞利桑那州新廠首期工程認列告一段落、後
續期程尚處規劃與等待階段，3Q26 營收佔比滑落至 0.50%；歐
洲市場與東南亞分別佔 2.20%與  1.60%。

個股報告

2 群益投顧 
  ESG：  
上品制定多項 ESG 目標，並逐年強化執行。2023 年，公司透過智能化水資
源管理系統，節省超過 27,300 噸的水，並在節能方面減少 184 萬度的電力
消耗。公司同時推動自發自用的太陽能發電計劃，預計 2024 年將建設 252kW
的太陽能系統，並在 2025 年進一步擴大其太陽能自發電的使用比例。 
公司簡介：  
上品專注於鐵氟龍內存設備的生產與加工，產品線涵蓋鐵氟龍內襯設備、管
件及應用材料等，累積長達 45 年氟素樹脂(鐵氟龍)材料改性與內襯設備製
造經驗，於 12/2021 正式掛牌上市。 
在產業鏈位置上，公司上游主要向國際氟化學大廠科慕 (Chemours) 與大金
(Daikin) 採購 PTFE、PFA、FEP 等原廠樹脂原料；中游則由上品完成兩階
段核心製造，先依不同氟素物性加工製成板材、管材、棒材及射出半成品，
再依據客戶廠區耐壓、耐溫及結構需求客製設計金屬設備外觀，最後以專利
內襯工法將氟材料緊密貼合於金屬內層。 
在半導體曝光微影、薄膜沉積、化學機械研磨 (CMP)、濕式清洗與蝕刻製
程中，液態電子化學品有 88% 屬於強腐蝕性酸鹼液體，包括硫酸
(H2SO4)、氫氟酸 (HF)、鹽酸 (HCl)、硝酸 (HNO3)、磷酸 (H3PO4)、氨水
(NH4OH) 與雙氧水 (H2O2)，另有 12% 為光阻液 (PR)、光阻稀釋液
(PGMEA/PGME) 與顯影液 (TMAH) 等有機溶劑。上品透過 TEFPASS® 
專利氟素塗層與內襯技術，確保上述高腐蝕化學品在儲運過程中達到極致
潔淨與近乎零金屬離子析出的嚴苛標準，直接保障先進製程晶圓良率，構築
極高的客戶認證門檻。 
3Q26 成長來自半導體產業需求，已成絕對核心成長引擎： 
3Q26 營收 15.98 億元 (QoQ+22.48%)，較  2Q26 的  13.05 億元顯著放量，
受惠於半導體晶圓廠建廠與擴產需求強勁，毛利率 34.64% (QoQ-0.98 ppt)，
主因第三季認列部分 2025 年承接之較低毛利訂單，微幅稀釋單季毛利表
現。 
在費用與營業槓桿方面，3Q26 費用為 1.54 億元 (QoQ-6.87%)，受惠於營
收規模大幅擴張，單季營業費用率顯著收斂至 9.63% (QoQ-3.04 ppt)，進而
推動營業利益達 4.00 億元 (QoQ+33.43%)，營益率逆勢擴張至 25.02% 
(QoQ+2.05 ppt)。  
業外與稅後獲利部分，3Q26 業外淨收益 0.16 億元(QoQ-11.71%），主要歸
因於利息收入增加及外匯匯兌變動。稅後淨利 3.41 億元，稅後 EPS4.27 元。
各業務部門、產品線及地區別營運表現： 
3Q26 半導體應用營收佔比 95.10%呈現逐季攀升態勢；石化化工產業佔比
縮減至 3.10%；面板與太陽能產業佔比為 1.40%，其他產業佔 0.40%。  
內襯設備 3Q26 佔比 69.70%，涵蓋半導體大宗化學品儲槽、反應槽及運送
槽車；其他材料佔比 16.50%，包含 PTFE 與  PFA 氟素半成品板材、管材
及精密射出件；內襯管件 (Lined Pipe & Fitting) 受惠於晶圓廠進入無塵室

長距離化學品輸送管網鋪設階段，佔比擴張至 10.20%；工程服務因現場裝

中國與台灣兩大市場合計貢獻 3Q26 營收 95.60%。其中中國市場受惠於

6 群益投顧 
  【投資評等說明】 
  評等 定義 
  強力買進(Strong Buy)  首次評等潛在上漲空間≥ 35%  
  買進(Buy)  15%≤首次評等潛在上漲空間<35%  
  區間操作(Trading Buy) 5%≤首次評等潛在上漲空間<15%

中立(Neutral)  
無法由基本面給予投資評等 
預期近期股價將處於盤整 
建議降低持股

【投資評等調整機制】 
  投資評等係「首次」給予特定個股投資評等時，其前一日收盤價相較 3 個月
目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為四個等級，定義如上。
爾後的投資評等係依循「首次評等」，直到停止推薦。  
「強力買進」、「買進」及「區間操作」均有 upside 目標價。差別僅在於，
首次評等時潛在報酬率不同。「中立」則無目標價。 
一旦我們給予特定個股「強力買進」、「買進」或「區間操作」之投資評等，
就是責任的開始，爾後將透過各式研究報告作定期性、持續性追蹤基本面及
股價變化，直到停止推薦。 
停止推薦情境： 
1. 達目標價。 
2. 雖未達原訂目標價，但檢視基本面、訊息面、籌碼面等多方訊息，研
判股價上漲空間已然有限，將適時出具降評報告。 
3. 推薦後股價不漲反跌。

【免責聲明】 
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」。我
們並不確保此資訊的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來預測
之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投資目的
或依預定對象之財務狀況所撰寫出來的，因此，此研究報告的目的，既非對
投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提供詢價服
務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值及收益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

新代(7750)：中國高階控制器需求持續強勁 
建議：評等買進

一、新代為台灣精密設備控制器
大廠 
 新代成立於 1995 年，為專注於精密設備控制器之大廠，以自有品牌 SYNTEC
行銷全球。主營產品涵蓋 CNC 數控系統、伺服產品與產業專機；集團亦積極佈
局新成長動能，旗下「聯達智能」與「正鉑雷射」分別專攻機器人/雲端智造
（IoT/MES）及工業減碳製程設備。公司具備兩大核心優勢：其一為精準的市場
定位，產品品質媲美德日大廠（如 Siemens、Fanuc），同時具備極佳的性價比，
成功填補高階進口與中低階國產間的市場缺口；其二為軟硬整合與在地服務，透
過橫跨歐美、亞太及中國 24 個分公司的全球據點，提供即時服務以協助機械廠
進行差異化升級。目前新代服務逾 2,000 家客戶，單一客戶營收占比均低於 5%，
客戶結構極具分散性與營運韌性，築起穩固的長期競爭護城河。

二、產品組合優化抵銷原物料成
本上漲，3Q26 毛利維持約 44%
水準 
 受惠中國工具機及金屬加工產業需求強勁、終端客戶持續升級高階控制器，以及
競爭對手關鍵零組件供應受限帶動轉單，新代 3Q26 營收達 78.05 億元，QoQ-
5.5%、YoY+157.7%，雖受傳統淡季影響較上季下滑，但仍創歷史單季次高。獲
利方面， 儘管記憶體價格上漲，受惠高階控制器銷售比重提升及產品組合優化，
3Q26 毛利率達 44.44%，季增 0.35 個百分點、年減 0.04 個百分點，維持公司
長期指引 45%±2%區間； 隨營收規模擴大及費用率改善，3Q26 營業利益達
21.06 億元，QoQ-11.9%、YoY+398.2%，營業利益率達 26.98%，年增 13.02
個百分點；稅後淨利達 16.80 億元，QoQ-11.4%、YoY+360.2%，單季 EPS 達
24.06 元，創歷史單季次高。1-3Q26 稅後淨利達 46.50 億元，YoY+221.7%，
EPS 達 66.59 元，反映高階產品需求成長、轉單效益及營運規模擴張持續挹注
獲利。

三、中國高階控制器滲透率持續
提升，海外布局與產能擴張挹注
2027 年成長動能 
 2026 年中國工具機產業維持成長動能，1–8 月金屬切削相關產業成長約 18–
19%，受惠製造業升級、五軸加工及車銑複合機等高階設備需求增加，帶動控制
器與伺服系統銷售。新代憑藉高階產品性能、較國際競品約 20%的價格優勢，
以及中國逾 2,000 家活躍客戶與在地直銷服務網絡，持續擴大市占率，2026 年
控制器出貨量預估 YoY+70–80%，並受惠日系競爭對手關鍵零組件供應受限，
取得大型工具機客戶轉單。儘管部分日系廠商供貨逐步恢復，公司透過提前備貨
及與記憶體原廠簽訂供應協議，預期關鍵零組件供應可維持至 2027 年下半年，
搭配產品組合優化及客戶黏著度提升，有望延續競爭優勢。海外布局方面，印度
市場受惠製造業在地化政策及工具機產業升級需求，帶動高階控制器國產替代
與供應商多元化商機。公司已與印度前十大工具機廠建立合作接洽，並取得兩筆
單筆超過 100 套的訂單，預期 2026 年印度營收 YoY+100%，惟目前營收貢獻

仍低；中長期目標五年內印度市占率提升至 10%，並持續擴大東南亞市場布局。
產能方面，蘇州二廠預計於 2027 年農曆年前後完成搬遷，馬來西亞新廠預計
1Q27 完工，隨新產能陸續投入，預計 1H27 整體產能將擴充至目前約 2.6 倍，
另有機械手臂產能增加，為後續營收成長提供支撐。展望 2027 年，管理層預期
中國工具機產業成長約 10–15%，公司目標營收成長 25–30%，並持續推動新代
控制器、聯達機械手臂及正鉑智慧工廠整合方案，搭配海外市場拓展及 AI 技術
導入，帶動營收與獲利持續成長。

四、評等買進理由  綜合前述，展望 2027 年，新代受惠中國高階工具機需求成長、日系競爭對手轉
單效益延續，以及控制器市占率持續提升，營運成長動能可望延續。隨蘇州二廠
及馬來西亞新廠陸續投產，預計自 2Q27 起新增產能逐步開出，整體控制器產能
有望擴充至目前約 2.5 倍，搭配印度大型工具機客戶導入加速，以及多合一高階
控制器與智慧自動化產品持續推出，有助推升產品 ASP 及獲利能力，預估 2026
年稅 EPS 為 91.49，預估 2027 年稅後 EPS 為 118.49。至於評價面部分，過往
3 年 PER 平均落在 25x-35x，目前約 16x，處歷史區間下緣，中國市占率提升、
印度市場拓展、新產能貢獻及智慧製造業務成長等中長期動能，認為目前評價具
吸引力，故給予買進評等，目標價 2300 元(25x2026EPS)。

투자의견 BUY  
매수, 유지 
6개월 목표주가 2,000,000 
유지  
현재주가 
(26.10.21) 
1,417,000 
제약업종

◼ 3Q26 파업 영향에도 컨센서스 상회 
◼ 4Q26 지연 물량 인식 및 신규 공장 기여로 실적 개선 전망 
◼ 투자의견 매수, 목표주가 2,000,000원 유지

투자의견 매수, 목표주가 2,000,000원 유지 
목표주가는 DCF 방식으로 산출(WACC 8.1%, 영구성장률 3.5% 적용). 기
존 공장의 높은 가동률을 기반으로 5공장 매출 기여 확대, 미국 생산거점 
확보, 신규 모달리티 진출을 통한 중장기 성장 지속 전망.

3Q26 Review: 파업 영향에도 컨센서스 상회  
3Q26 매출액 1조 4,903억원(+18.5% yoy, +12.8% qoq), 영업이익 6,401
억원(+1.1% yoy, +9.1% qoq), 영업이익률 42.9%(-7.5%p yoy)를 기록하며 
당사 추정치 및 시장 컨센서스 상회. 5월 1차 파업에 따른 매출 차질(약 
1,500억원) 에도 불구하고, 기존 1~4공장 Full 가동이 지속되며 견조한 실
적 달성. 5공장 PPQ 배치 생산 확대와 및 미국 록빌 공장 매출 인식도 외
형 성장에 기여. 다만, 신규 생산시설 초기 가동비와 제품 믹스 영향으로 
영업이익률은 전년 동기 대비 하락.

4Q26 매출액 1조 5,764억원(+22.6% yoy), 영업이익 7,075억원
(+34.0% yoy) 전망  
4Q26에는 2분기 파업으로 지연된 생산물량의 순차적인 매출 인식과 미국 
록빌 공장 매출 기여, 5공장 PPQ 배치 생산 확대로 실적 개선 예상, 동사
는 신규 생산시설의 매출 가시성 확보를 바탕으로 2026년 매출 성장률 
20%를 무난히 달성할 것으로 전망(연초 가이던스 15~20%).

신규 수주 회복과 포트폴리오 확장에 주목 
미국 의약품 관세정책 불확실성 완화로 지연됐던 수주 협상이 재개되며 신
규 수주 환경 개선 중. 3Q26 약 3억달러의 신규 수주를 확보했으며, 대형 
계약 협상도 구체화되고 있어 2027년 수주 회복 기대. 이는 5공장 가동률 
확대와 중장기 성장 가시성을 높이는 요인. 6공장은 ‘26년말~’27년초 착
공을 목표로 기존 증설 일정 유지. 
폴리펩타이드 그룹 인수는 1차 공개매수 응모율 87.88%를 기록하며 연내 
인수 완료 추진 중. 인수 완료 시 펩타이드 CDMO로 사업영역이 확대되며 
글로벌 생산거점 및 고객 기반 다변화 기대.

산업 투자의견 
- Overweight(비중확대)

- Neutral(중립) 
  : 향후 6개월간 업종지수상승률이 시장수익률과 유사한 수준 예상 
- Underweight(비중축소) 
  : 향후 6개월간 업종지수상승률이 시장수익률 대비 하회 예상

- Buy(매수)  
  : 향후 6개월간 시장수익률 대비 10%p 이상 주가 상승 예상 
- Marketperform(시장수익률) 
  : 향후 6개월간 시장수익률 대비 -10%p~10%p 주가 변동 예상

Document 254: A primer on fiscal measures debated in French elections_ Most candidates commit to restoring fiscal sustainability, with uneven credibility (2026-11-23)

Source PDF: all report pdf/A primer on fiscal measures debated in French elections_ Most candidates commit to restoring fiscal sustainability, with uneven credibility (2026-11-23).pdf
Report source: BNP Paribas
Date: 2026-11-23
Pages: 3
Status: ok

### Page 1

The bank
for a changing
world
7
economic-research.bnpparibas.comEcoWeek 26.43 / 23 November 2026FOCUS — FRENCH ELECTIONS: A PRIMER ON FISCAL MEASURES
A PRIMER ON FISCAL MEASURES DEBATED IN FRENCH ELECTIONS: MOST CANDIDATES COMMIT TO RESTORING FISCAL
SUSTAINABILITY, WITH UNEVEN CREDIBILITY
1 Other growth-critical policy proposals will be reviewed in thematic deep-dives to be published over the coming months.
This note offers an overview of the measures proposed by presidential election candidates from the main political groups,

namely Marine Le Pen, Jean-Luc Mélenchon, Édouard Philippe, Raphaël Glucksmann, David Lisnard and Bruno Retailleau.
The focus here is on their proposals for public finances and pensions1. Overall, budget plans go in the right direction but
rely on overly optimistic costs and revenue assumptions. However, in several cases a proposed “golden rule” enhances
credibility. The proposals regarding pensions improve on the status quo in most cases, including, in some cases, by intro-

ducing a capitalisation pillar. However, most safeguard costly features of the current system, without enough measures to
offset their impact fully.
KEY TAKEAWAYS ON PUBLIC FINANCES (TABLE 1)
- The bulk of the candidates target a below 3% of GDP fiscal deficit by 2032. The budget proposals are often optimistic (or lack sufficient detail),
which is a common feature of previous presidential campaigns (e.g., in 2017, E. Macron proposed reducing public spending to 50% of GDP by

2022).
- A majority of candidates propose a “golden rule”, including the National Rally (Rassemblement national). A rule would lend credibility to their
fiscal path (if the planned measures are insufficient, the golden rule would require them to adopt additional measures, as it was done e.g. in
Germany under the debt brake).
- The budget announcements made by several candidates — including the National Rally (RN) and La France Insoumise (LFI) — are expected

to worsen the fiscal deficit in 2027. According to our calculations, a one-year delay in fiscal consolidation would result in a 2 pp increase in the
public debt-to-GDP ratio by the end of that consolidation (in 2032, debt would reach 126.5% of GDP instead of 124.5% without this delay), and
even more if the deficit or interest rate conditions were to deteriorate significantly.
- In the absence of a golden rule, LFI’s commitment to fiscal consolidation remains uncertain. The candidate’s promises are very costly, leading

to a clear increase in the fiscal deficit. Some of the proposed measures are confiscatory in nature and would likely shrink the tax base. The can-
didate proposes no binding safeguards to ensure that the deficit does not spiral out of control. Furthermore, his proposal to write off part of the
public debt held by the ECB could be interpreted as a way to avoid having to undertake fiscal consolidation efforts to stabilise public debt (not
to mention its feasibility).

KEY TAKEAWAYS ON PENSIONS  (TABLE 2)
- The candidates’ positions on pensions reflect real, underlying progress, particularly in order to mitigate the impact of the repeal of the Borne
reform (retirement age back to 62 years old) through savings on other parameters (contribution period, or tightened conditions such as an in-
creased discount per missing quarter).
- The prospect of fully indexing pensions, as proposed in several candidates' platforms, would be very costly (nearly 2% of GDP).

- Lowering the retirement age and full indexation undermine the system’s long-term financial stability. Everything else equal, they also reduce
the size of the active labour force, a headwind to growth and fiscal sustainability.
- Édouard Philippe, David Lisnard and Bruno Retailleau advocate raising the retirement age to 65, and Édouard Philippe also calls for extending
the contribution period to 45 years (while maintaining more favourable treatment for long careers and physically demanding jobs). The savings

associated with extending working careers represent a significant contribution to the announced fiscal consolidation effort.
- With regard to capitalisation, there are marked differences between the candidates who currently rule it out (J.L. Mélenchon, R. Glucksmann)
and the others, who have all made proposals along these lines (including the RN).

### Page 1

Adobe预测，11⽉⾄12⽉"先买后付"⽀出将达213亿美元，同⽐增⻓6.6%；其中82%通过
⼿机完成。整体假⽇在线总⽀出预计达2751亿美元，增⻓6.7%。与此同时，AI购物⼯具引
导流量预计激增130%，正深刻重塑消费者购物路径。
摘要：
美国消费者在今年假⽇购物季的借贷消费规模预计将创下历史新⾼，“先买后付”（BNPL）⼯具的
使⽤量持续攀升。
据Adobe预测，在11⽉1⽇⾄12⽉31⽇期间，美国消费者使⽤“先买后付”的在线借贷消费⽀出将
达到创纪录的213亿美元，较去年同期增⻓6.6%。
仅在“⽹购星期⼀”（Cyber Monday），该项⽀出预计将达到10.9亿美元，连续第⼆年突破10亿
美元⼤关；“⿊五”（Black Friday）也将额外贡献8.07亿美元的信贷消费。
这⼀⽀付模式的显著特征在于对智能⼿机的极度依赖。Adobe的数据显⽰，⾼达82%的“先买后
付”⽀出将通过⼿机完成，机构将这⼀现象直接归因于移动设备更容易触发消费者的“冲动购物”
⾏为。
上⼀个假⽇季，“先买后付”已推动了200亿美元的在线⽀出，增幅近10%。

这⼀创纪录的信贷消费规模，建⽴在整体假⽇在线⽀出稳步扩张的基础之上。在深度折扣的刺
激下，消费者的购物需求正呈现出新的特征，市场整体流量与购买转化率受到密切关注。
整体假⽇在线⽀出稳步扩张
“先买后付”数据的增⻓折射出更庞⼤的零售基数。Adobe预计，今年假⽇季美国在线总⽀出将达
到2751亿美元，同⽐增⻓6.7%。
其中，从感恩节到“⽹购星期⼀”的“⽹络周”（Cyber Week）五天时间内，预计将带来475亿美元
的销售额，同⽐增⻓7.4%，占整个假⽇季⽀出的17.3%。
从单⽇表现来看，“⽹购星期⼀”仍将是整个购物季中销售规模最⼤的⼀天，预计达151亿美元，
增幅6.2%；⽽“⿊五”的增速更为迅猛，预计增⻓9.2%⾄129亿美元。

此外，感恩节当天的在线销售额预计增⻓8.5%⾄69亿美元，其中63%的交易料将通过移动设备
完成，创下历史新⾼，⾼于去年同期的61.6%，也⾼于整个购物季57.4%的平均⽔平。
深度折扣推动⽇常必需品热销
整体销售额的增⻓部分得益于年内最⼤⼒度的价格让利。
Adobe指出，“⿊五”的强劲增⻓在⼀定程度上归功于电视、服装和家电等品类的极深折扣。“⽹
络周”期间的整体折扣⼒度预计最⾼可达标价的30%。
从促销节奏来看，电⼦产品和电脑在“⽹购星期⼀”降价幅度最⼤；电视、玩具、服装、家电和
家具在“⿊五”折扣最⼤；⽽体育⽤品的最佳买点则在感恩节。
引⼈关注的是，⼤量假⽇消费并未流向传统的节⽇礼物。消费者正利⽤折扣⼤幅囤积⽇常必需
品。与9⽉份的平均⽔平相⽐，“⽹络周”期间个⼈卫⽣⽤品的在线销量预计将激增150%，基础服

装飙升210%，婴⼉⽤品增⻓113%，宠物⽤品增⻓93%，家庭清洁⽤品增⻓49%。
信贷隐患与AI购物⼯具的崛起
随着“先买后付”在⽇常消费中的渗透，潜在的信贷压⼒正在显现。
据LendingTree去年的调查数据显⽰，⾼达四分之⼀的“先买后付”⽤⼾使⽤该贷款购买杂货，这
⼀⽐例⾼于前⼀年的14%；同时，41%的⽤⼾表⽰在过去⼀年中存在逾期还款记录，⾼于此前
的34%。
在⽀付⽅式演变的同时，AI正在深刻改变消费者的购物路径。
Adobe预计，今年假⽇季通过AI⼯具链接引导⾄美国零售⽹站的流量将飙升130%。其中，感恩
节的AI引导流量将⼤涨159%，“⿊五”和“⽹购星期⼀”也将分别上涨95%和88%。
在Adobe对5000名消费者的调查中，77%使⽤过AI购物的受访者表⽰对其购买决策感到更有信
⼼。
上述预测基于Adobe截⾄9⽉28⽇的数据模型，实际零售数据将在⻓周末期间陆续落地。作为参
考，去年假⽇购物季的实际在线销售额达到创纪录的2578亿美元，最终超出Adobe当时的预测
值44亿美元。

Document 259: AI估值被严重误判！贝莱德基金经理驳斥“见顶论”：Anthropic上市标志着应用层大爆发

Source PDF: all report pdf/AI估值被严重误判！贝莱德基金经理驳斥“见顶论”：Anthropic上市标志着应用层大爆发.pdf
Report source: Unknown
Date: 未知日期
Pages: 5
Status: ok

### Page 1

⻉莱德AI主题ETF基⾦经理Tony Kim认为，市场严重误判了AI估值：芯⽚硬件股盈利⾼增
却已跌⾄低于标普500的⽔平，背后是"2027年⻅顶、2028年下滑"的⼀致预期。模型能⼒
每年提升⼗倍、落地速度远未跟上，AI交易正从算⼒层切向应⽤层，Anthropic融资事件是
分⽔岭；五年后机器流量将占互联⽹99%，Agent⾝份安全与数据层是最先受益的环节。
摘要：
管理着约140亿美元⻉莱德AI主题ETF的Tony Kim，对当下华尔街最流⾏的⼀个判断给出了截然
相反的答案。
他在彭博《Trillions》播客中认为，市场正在为"AI资本开⽀2027年⻅顶、2028年⾛平甚⾄下
滑"定价，直接后果是——芯⽚与硬件公司的盈利在成倍增⻓，股价却在跌，估值已经低于标普
500整体⽔平。"我确实认为市场在向下超调。"
在他看来，这不是估值分歧，⽽是对"变化速率"的系统性低估：模型能⼒每年提升约⼀个数量
级，⽽现实经济的采⽤速度远远落后于这条曲线。
他把Anthropic的⼀级市场融资事件视为分⽔岭——AI交易正从"建算⼒"切换到"跑应⽤"。

芯⽚股⽐⼤盘还便宜，市场在给⼀个"2027⻅顶"的剧本定价
Kim 的第⼀层论证从估值结构切⼊。他把当下的科技板块拆成两块：芯⽚与硬件，以及超⼤
⼚。
原因不在基本⾯，在预期。
他把这称作当前最⼤的分歧点："要么你相信AI会继续下去，那这些东西便宜得离谱；要么你相
信AI会⻅顶回落，那市场的恐惧就是合理的。"
超⼤⼚同样被下杀了。Kim 认为，⾕歌、微软、Meta 这类公司正在把业务的根本重⼼转向算⼒
基础设施提供⽅，"它们把每⼀块钱利润都投进资本开⽀，⾃由现⾦流因此掉下来甚⾄转负，估
值⾃然被打下来"。他的判断是这类公司的资本开⽀还要再烧"两三年"，之后才会迎来回报。
⾄于市场为何在此时杀估值，他归因于融资能⼒与投资回报两个担忧叠加。今年约⼀万亿美元
的资本开⽀中，"85%到90%来⾃最⼤的那⼏家公司，只有10%到15%落在那些资产负债表不够
厚的第⼆梯队新云⼚商⾝上"，⽽这部分恰恰是市场最担⼼的环节，英伟达还是其中最⼤的⽀持
者之⼀。
"这些公司现在的估值⽐标普500还便宜。我知道这听起来很离谱。它们的盈利在成倍增⻓，股

价却在往下⾛，所以市盈率越压越低。"
"市场认为AI会在2027年⻅顶。⽽且⼀种越来越成形的看法是，2028年会⾛平甚⾄下滑。"
"我认为市场对这⻔⽣意的融资能⼒做了过度修正。"
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

⼗倍年增速与落地速度之间的裂缝
⽀撑 Kim 逆向判断的核⼼，是⼀个被他反复强调的概念：变化的速率。
他提到，随着"递归⾃我改进"这类机制出现，这条曲线的斜率还可能更陡。但真正的⽭盾在于另
⼀端：能⼒在⻜跃，采⽤在爬⾏。
这条裂缝正是他解释"看不到ROI"质疑的钥匙："⼤家说这项投资看不到回报，我认为它最终会显
现出来，只是也许不是现在，是明年、后年。"
同样的逻辑也解释了为什么他期待应⽤层。Kim 把AI拆成两层：⼀层是"原⼦"——电⼒、芯
⽚、服务器、云、超⼤⼚构成的计算与基础设施层；另⼀层是"电⼦"——软件与服务构成的应⽤
层。过去四年，全部故事都发⽣在第⼀层。
Agent 数量将百倍于⼈⼝，安全与数据是最先被点燃的环节
Kim 对应⽤层最先落地的⽅向，给出了⼀个反直觉的排序：⽹络安全。
他持仓中的 Okta 是典型案例。这家做⾝份安全的公司，核⼼业务是为⼈类提供⾝份认证，⼏年
前⾼增⻓，之后增速回落到个位数，"AI来了之后，2023到2025年这块核⼼业务并没有加速"。
转折发⽣在今年。

他给了⼀个量级对⽐：地球上有80亿⼈，⽽机器⾝份的数量最终可能是"上千亿"。
第⼆条线索是数据层。Kim ⽤⼀句话概括了他眼中未来企业的形态：
"模型能⼒⼤概⼀年提升⼀个数量级。如果算⼗年，就是⼀万倍；再⼗年，⼜是⼀万倍。这是⾮
线性的。"
"模型能⼒的增⻓速度，快于它在现实经济中的扩散速度。"
"这次Anthropic的上市，第⼀次出现⼀家纯粹的应⽤公司，也许它会开启AI的下⼀个阶段，让我
们⾛得⽐算⼒更宽。"
"现在有了Agent，它们不是⼈，是机器。今年出了这么多安全事件，从Hugging Face那次攻击
开始，⼤家意识到这些Agent可能会失控。所以你需要的不只是⼈类⾝份的安全，还有机器⾝
份、Agent⾝份的安全。"
"这个变化今年才刚刚开始，还处在极早期的阶段。"
"你会接⼊智能，选⼀个模型；中间是彭博、⻉莱德这样的数据层；上⾯跑着Agent。未来的公司
就⻓这样，Agent和你的数据交互，你往⾥灌原始智能。"

### Page 3

沿着这个图景，⻉莱德在数据管道、Agent构建、Agent防护三个环节上布了仓。他把这称作"企
业整体重构的第⼀局"。
99%的流量将由机器产⽣，⽽市场还在赌算⼒⻅顶
Kim 引⽤了 Cloudflare 的数据：今年年初，AI与机器流量已经超过⼈类流量。
他的推演是：每个⼈未来会有⼗个、甚⾄上百个Agent昼夜不停地⼯作。⼈类的刷⼿机流量在总
量⾥会变得微不⾜道，⽽更耗算⼒的是那些⾮⼈类的负载——药物研发、材料发现、化学与⽣
物学、机器⼈系统训练，"可能会有⼀整个数据中⼼只做药物发现"。
这正好回到他对市场定价的批评：
⾄于AI交易的新阶段怎么布局，Kim 的判断是软件、服务与AI模型将三合⼀，"以结果的形式交
付"。
他以⾃⼰的⼯作流举例：⽇历、邮件接⼊AI助⼿，每天处理两千封邮件、按优先级分流，再分设
多个Agent盯半导体、数据中⼼、基因组学，每周⽣成⼀份"AI在⽣物学领域的进展"⻓报告。

"如果今天机器和⼈类流量是六⽐四，五年后会是99⽐1。"
"越来越多的算⼒负载不是由⼈产⽣的，事实上99%都将是⾮⼈类的。"
"市场在说算⼒会在2028年⻅顶并下滑。这与即将到来的东西完全相反——所需的算⼒会⽐我们
现在设想的还要⾼出好⼏个数量级。这就是最⼤的分歧所在。"
"把AI模型、软件、服务这三样合成⼀个东西，以结果交付。然后把这个想法套⽤到标普的每⼀
个⾏业上。"
"现在还做不到让它替我把税报了，但⽅向是这样。"

### Page 4

⻛险提⽰及免责条款
市场有⻛险，投资需谨慎。本⽂不构成个⼈投资建议，也未考虑到个别⽤⼾特殊的投资⽬标、财务状况或需要。⽤⼾应考虑本⽂
中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。

Document 266: AI股抛售潮接近尾声？摩根大通警告：真正的风险可能在2027年

Source PDF: all report pdf/AI股抛售潮接近尾声？摩根大通警告：真正的风险可能在2027年.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-11-27
Pages: 2
Status: ok

### Page 1

摩根⼤通认为，AI股抛售或已接近尾声，美股市场有望迎来短期反弹，但这并⾮调整结
束。投资者情绪已降⾄20年来极端低位，与基本⾯表现出现背离。该⾏警告，2027年经济
增⻓放缓叠加央⾏持续加息，可能引发政策失误，推动市场进⼊更持久的下⾏阶段，甚⾄
跌破当前低点。
摘要：
AI股的抛售潮或许正在接近尾声，但市场真正需要警惕的，可能不是眼前的暴跌，⽽是反弹之后
逐渐累积的经济增⻓⻛险。
摩根⼤通欧洲量化策略主管Khuram Chaudhry领衔的团队在最新报告中指出，当前市场正处于
“熊市三阶段”框架中的第⼀阶段（崩跌）尾声，短期有望转向第⼆阶段（反弹），但随后可能进
⼊第三阶段（缓慢下⾏），2027年全球经济增⻓放缓将成为市场⾯临的主要⻛险。
该团队认为，近期动量股的集中抛售已显现出熊市第⼀阶段的典型特征，⽽投资者情绪跌⾄过
去20年来的极端低位，与盈利预期、采购经理⼈指数等基本⾯指标所反映的情况出现明显背
离，短期反弹的条件正在形成。
但反弹并不意味着⻛险解除。摩根⼤通警告，如果经济增⻓已经趋缓，央⾏仍坚持收紧货币政

策，政策与经济周期之间的错配可能加剧增⻓放缓，推动市场进⼊更持久的下⾏阶段，甚⾄跌
破当前低点。
崩跌接近尾声，市场或迎来反弹
摩根⼤通团队将熊市划分为三个阶段：崩跌、反弹和缓慢下⾏。这⼀框架曾在互联⽹泡沫破
裂、全球⾦融危机、疫情冲击以及2022年加息周期与俄乌冲突期间得到验证。
该团队表⽰，从历史经验来看，整个过程平均持续约18个⽉。近期动量股的集中抛售，可能是
第⼀阶段崩跌⾏情的再次显现，⽽市场向第⼆阶段反弹的切换或将很快到来。
情绪指标也为这⼀判断提供了⽀撑。摩根⼤通⾃有的全球投资者情绪指标显⽰，⽬前市场情绪
已降⾄过去20年来极为低迷的⽔平，类似情况仅出现过五次，且均发⽣在重⼤增⻓冲击期间。
与此同时，分析师盈利预期和采购经理⼈指数（PMI）等基本⾯指标，并未显⽰经济前景需要如
此悲观的定价。情绪与基本⾯之间的落差，进⼀步增强了短期反弹的可能性。
增⻓放缓叠加政策收紧，2027年⻛险升温
不过，摩根⼤通认为，反弹并不意味着市场调整已经结束。随着市场进⼊第三阶段——“缓慢下
⾏”，经济增⻓放缓可能成为2027年全球市场⾯临的主要⻛险。

从先⾏指标来看，摩根⼤通美国量化宏观指数仍处于扩张区间，但已接近历史上通常出现均值
回归的⽔平。美国供应管理协会（ISM）制造业指数中的订单与库存⽐率也已⻅顶，显⽰增⻓动
能可能正在减弱。
更值得警惕的是货币政策与经济增⻓之间的错配。该团队指出，⽬前增⻓脆弱性尚未成为市场
共识，市场定价仍反映出⼀季度将有超过两次加息的预期。

Topics  Regulatory reform in India's insurance sector

Over the past quarter century, the Indian government has pursued the liberalization of the country's insurance market, 
with the aim of bringing stability to people's living standards and strengthening financial intermediation functions. 
Recently, however, it has begun to reverse course, tightening rules in order to better protect consumers.

■ India's insurance market has grown rapidly with deregulation 
The Indian government has long pursued 
the development of the country's insurance 
market, with the aim of bringing stability to 
people's living standards and strengthening 
financial intermediation functions. This 
stance can be attributed to two background 
factors:  
The first is that India's social security 
system remains underdeveloped. As a result, 
enrollment in health and life insurance can 
provide a vital safety net for mitigating the 
risk of sharp declines in household income 
due to unexpected illnesses or accidents.  
The second is that the sustainable 
development of the Indian economy requires 
the accumulation of long-term and stable 
domestic capital, separate from short-term 
bank deposits, in order to finance the 
development of infrastructure and the 
nurturing of industry. The hope has been that 
the enormous sums of money collected and 
managed by insurance companies can serve 
as a reliable source for this long-term 
financing.  
These factors have led the Indian 
government to walk a path of gradual 
deregulation over the past quarter century. 
The process began with the liberalization of 
market entry by private and foreign 
companies in 2000, which was followed by 
a lifting of the ban on "bancassurance" (the 
sale of insurance through bank branches) in 
2002, the raising of the ceiling on foreign 
ownership, and other reforms.  
This series of deregulatory measures 
spurred the entry of numerous private and foreign 
firms, causing market competition to intensify. Several 
Japanese insurers, including Nippon Life and the Tokio 
Marine Group, also entered the market, helping to drive 
investment in India.  
Besides deregulation related to market entry and 
sales, other factors also contributed to the rapid growth 
of India's insurance market, namely the expansion of 
the middle class, improvements in household financial 
literacy, and the digitalization of insurance services. 
Premium income grew at a pace exceeding economic 
growth, and in recent years insurance companies' assets 
under management have reached a level equivalent to 
Year Event

and Development Authority of India)

3 
incentives to sell products and inappropriate sales practices. This should shore up the credibility of the 
insurance market and the foundation for its sustainable development. 
On the minus side, there are worries about side effects arising from the transition. As rules on selling 
become stricter, insurance companies and the financial institutions acting as insurance agents will inevitably 
see some temporary moderation in the pace of their earnings growth. Moreover, given that insurance 
companies and financial institutions are major investors in India's financial markets, tighter regulation is 
likely to exert downward pressure on equity and bond prices. 
These changes in the insurance market provide an important perspective for assessing the future 
trajectory of the Indian economy. They illustrate that rapid market expansion driven by liberalization can 
create a variety of problems. And this dynamic is not limited to the insurance sector, as it can also occur 
across many other industries. At the same time, the redesign and recalibration of regulations in response to 
such developments can provide a foundation for a new stage of growth. The new insurance regulations 
could constitute an early example of this phenomenon, so will be closely watched to see what changes they 
bring in the years ahead.  
  (Shotaro Kumagai)

Topics  Talent travel curbs may weigh on China’s growth

In September, the Chinese government introduced new exit-entry rules. Indicative of an effort to retain talent in order 
to prevent technology leakage, the rules could narrow opportunities for China to absorb overseas knowledge and weigh 
on its economic growth over the medium to long term.

■ Scope of overseas travel restrictions expanded 
On September 15, the Chinese 
government brought in new rules 
specifying conditions and screening 
procedures for entry and exit. The new 
rules explicitly state that the Ministry of 
Commerce or other departments may 
prohibit Chinese citizens from leaving the 
country if they violate export-control or 
technology import/export regulations and 
are deemed likely to threaten national 
industrial or technological security.  
Under China's Exit and Entry 
Administration Law, which came into 
effect in 2013, defendants and suspects in 
criminal cases, as well as persons involved 
in unsettled civil cases whom courts have 
prohibited from leaving the country, had 
already been subject to exit restrictions. In 
addition, Chinese citizens could be denied 
permission to leave the country if the 
competent departments determined that 
their departure might endanger national 
security or interests. Aside from formal exit 
bans, personnel who handle state secrets 
and officials in the public sector have long 
been subject to controls such as centralized 
passport management and the need to 
obtain approval in advance of overseas travel. What the new rules effectively do is make it clear that exit 
can also be restricted when there are issues from the standpoint of economic security. 
Against this backdrop, it was reported at the end of September that overseas travel restrictions already 
imposed on key personnel in the AI and semiconductor sectors had been expanded to include their spouses, 
children, and other family members. According to these reports, these key personnel include prominent AI 
startup founders, senior executives at major private enterprises, and core R&D talent. Although the measure 
does not constitute a blanket travel ban, affected individuals and their families are reportedly required to 
obtain government approval for exit even in the case of short private trips or business travel. 
This regulatory tightening is thought to be related to the technological rivalry between the U.S. and China. 
As competition surrounding critical technologies such as generative AI intensifies, the Chinese government 
appears to view not only technologies themselves, but also the researchers and engineers who create them, 
as strategic assets. By widening travel curbs to encompass family members, the authorities are likely 
seeking to prevent overseas emigration by individuals or the relocation of R&D operations abroad. A 
noteworthy aspect of the recent reports is that the controls apparently now extend beyond the public sector 
to key personnel at private enterprises, as well as their families. Restrictions like these on talent mobility 
could undermine the mechanisms of technology absorption and productivity improvement that have 
hitherto facilitated China's growth.

### Page 6

ASIA MONTHLY REPORT  November 2026, No.308

The Japan Research Institute, Limited  
Economics Department

5 
■ China's productivity gains have been supported by market opening and technology 
absorption 
Productivity exerts a substantial influence on 
economic growth. Total factor productivity (TFP), an 
indicator of an economy's overall production 
efficiency, provides an estimate of efficiency in 
production that cannot be explained solely in terms of 
inputs of capital and labor, and reflects factors such as 
levels of technology and efficiency of resource 
allocation. 
China's TFP has been rising for many years. In 
addition to the transition to a market economy and the 
growth of private enterprises since the country adopted 
the policy of reform and opening up, Chinese 
productivity has benefitted from the absorption of 
foreign technology and managerial know‑how through 
trade and direct investment. A labor shift from 
agriculture to manufacturing and other sectors, along 
with competition among firms and the weeding out of 
weaker players, has also helped deliver productivity 
gains across the economy.  
Nevertheless, China's TFP remains low relative to 
other major economies. According to estimates from 
Penn World Table, China's TFP in 2023 was less than 
half that of the U.S., and it was also below the levels not 
only of Germany and other major European economies 
and of South Korea and Japan, but of Thailand and 
Malaysia, too. Factors behind this include inefficient 
resource allocation and the slow diffusion of advanced 
technologies and management practices. Preferential 
financial and institutional treatment for state‑owned 
enterprises, regional protectionism, and mechanisms 
that impede market entry and exit have distorted the 
allocation of resources, preventing capital and labor 
from being fully utilized by more productive firms and sectors. Moreover, although China has achieved 
strong competitiveness in certain advanced industries, such as electric vehicles and semiconductors, the 
leading-edge technologies and management practices developed in these sectors have not sufficiently 
diffused to the service sector and small and medium‑sized enterprises, which form the foundation of the 
economy, and this has constrained overall productivity growth.  
■ Moves to contain technology could hinder growth over the medium to long term 
Efforts to retain talent in order to prevent technology leakage could further delay China's productivity 
catch‑up with advanced economies. While restrictions on overseas travel by elite professionals may help 
with the retention of domestic R&D talent and protection of current technological advantages, they could 
also reduce opportunities to absorb overseas knowledge and to jointly develop new technologies with 
foreign partners. The transfer of technology and know‑how does not occur solely through the sharing of 
"explicit knowledge," in the form of academic papers, technical blueprints, and so on. "Tacit knowledge," 
which is transmitted through face‑to‑face discussion, collaborative work, and the acquisition of experience 
in production settings, is also crucial. If travel controls delay or prevent participation in international 
conferences, joint research projects, and technology exchanges with overseas companies, opportunities to 
absorb such knowledge may be lost, slowing technological innovation and improvements in production 
processes. Furthermore, if obtaining travel approval becomes more time‑consuming or less predictable, the 
overseas assignment or international deployment of technical personnel could also be obstructed. 
The new rules also cover the entry of foreign nationals. If uncertainty surrounding the administration of 
entry and exit controls increases, it could create obstacles to attracting and retaining highly skilled talent 
from abroad. At a time when inefficient resource allocation and the slow diffusion of advanced knowledge 
are already weighing on productivity growth in China, curbs on people-to- people exchange could further

impede both the absorption and creation of knowledge, as well as its practical application. Although the 
magnitude of the impact will depend on the scope of application and actual implementation on the ground, 
restrictions on talent exchanges and overseas travel that reflect excessive emphasis on technology 
protection could slow China's TFP growth over the medium to long term and act as a headwind to the 
Chinese economy by lowering its potential growth rate. 
(Hiromu Komiya)

Top 5 ngành tăng / giảm  VNINDEX giảm điểm trong tuần qua, -32.53 điểm tương ứng -1.83%, với thanh khoản suy 
giảm. Linh kiện ô tô, Dầu khí và Công nghệ là các nhóm ngành có mức giảm rõ rệt nhất 
trong tuần. Khối ngoại duy trì bán ròng so với xu hướng của tuần trước với giá trị -634 tỷ 
đồng, tập trung ở các mã FPT, ACB và VPL; mặt khác, tự doanh đảo chiều bán ròng ở mức 
-609 tỷ đồng.

Ở một khía cạnh khác, khối ngoại đảo chiều bán ròng -5,829 HĐTL VN30F1M trong tuần 
qua và đang duy trì vị thế bán lũy kế -1,462 hợp đồng. Tự doanh duy trì mua ròng 722 hợp 
đồng nhưng đang duy trì trạng thái lũy kế bán ròng -5,278 hợp đồng.

TTCK Việt Nam đánh mất đà hồi phục của tuần trước, khi VN-Index quay đầu giảm khoảng 
1,8% về đóng cửa quanh 1.742 điểm, với phiên cuối tuần có thời điểm lùi sâu về sát 1.727 
điểm trước khi thu hẹp đà giảm. Dù mùa báo cáo KQKD quý 3 diễn ra tương đối tích cực, 
tuy nhiên các lo ngại về việc môi trường lãi suất cao vẫn duy trì trong bối cảnh căng thẳng 
Trung Đông leo thang đang đè nặng lên tâm lý nhà đầu tư. Kết quả kỳ họp của FED trong 
tuần qua nhìn chung phù hợp với kỳ vọng và không gây ra ảnh hưởng đáng kể. Điểm kém 
tích cực nhất là khối ngoại đã quay lại bán ròng với tổng giá trị khoảng 2.700 tỷ đồng 
trong tuần — đảo ngược diễn biến mua ròng cuối tuần trước, cho thấy đà rút vốn ngoại 
vẫn chưa thực sự dừng lại.

Ngưỡng kỹ thuật

VNINDEX tuần qua hình thành mẫu nến giảm trên đồ thị tuần, nhưng chưa thoái lui quá 1 
nửa cây nến tăng của tuần trước, cùng thanh khoản được tiết giảm, hàm ý nhịp điều chỉnh 
vẫn trong phạm vi cho phép. Trên khung ngày, sau bốn phiên giằng co đầu tuần với thanh 
khoản thấp, chỉ số đã chịu áp lực sụt giảm mạnh hơn trong phiên cuối tuần, chớm xuống 
dưới đường tín hiệu chính giữa dải Bollinger, nhưng cho tín hiệu rút chân ngay sau đó, thể 
hiện áp lực bán tháo đã tạm thời được kiểm soát. Mặc dù rủi ro mở rộng thêm nhịp điều 
chỉnh vẫn còn để ngỏ, tuy nhiên khi xu hướng tăng ngắn hạn đang được bảo lưu, VNINDEX 
được kỳ vọng sẽ cho phản ứng hồi phục trở lại tại các điểm đỡ bên dưới.

(Ghi chú: NĐT có thể dựa theo nhận định xu hướng thị trường để tham khảo khuyến nghị 
hành động tương ứng trong bảng phía trên)

Danh mục đầu tư mẫu

Phương pháp tiếp cận: 
- Danh mục tập hợp các cổ phiếu tiềm năng nhất được KBSV 
lựa chọn và tái cơ cấu theo tháng. 
- Ngưỡng cắt lỗ cần được cân nhắc cho mỗi mã là -15%. 
- Danh mục giả định luôn duy trì tỷ trọng 100% là cổ phiếu với 
trọng số được phân bổ đều giữa các mã.

Lưu ý:  
- NĐT được khuyến nghị mua tại mức giá bằng hoặc thấp hơn 
giá khuyến nghị mua. 
- Lợi nhuận kỳ vọng được tính theo giá khuyến nghị mua. 
 So sánh hiệu suất với VN30 Index

Document 302: Cliff Notes_ in pursuit of future gain (2026-11-28)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Cliff Notes_ in pursuit of future gain (2026-11-28).pdf
Report source: Westpac IQ
Date: 2026-11-28
Pages: 10
Status: ok

### Page 1

#cliffnotes #rba #australia #inflation #us #fomc #ecb #aud #usd
Cliff Notes: in pursuit of future gain
By Ryan Wells, Elliot Clarke & Illiana Jain
10:02 November 27 2026 Share
Key insights from the week that was.
The main event in Australia this week was the October CPI report which surprised modestly to the
upside, headline inflation rising 0.5% (4.9%yr). The main drivers were auto fuel (+6.2%), domestic
holiday travel (+4.5%) and new dwelling costs (+0.8%). Fuelled by some project home builders

reducing discounts, the increase in new dwelling costs was noteworthy; however, subdued demand
should see discounting return. More broadly, underlying inflation continued to accelerate. Trimmed

### Page 2

mean inflation gained 0.5% in the month and 3.8%yr, a cycle high that breaks the 3.5–3.6%yr range
held since May. The six-month annualised pulse was stronger still at 4.3%yr.
As noted by Westpac Chief Economist Luci Ellis following the release, October likely marks the peak
for inflation, but the upside surprise in dwellings and other early-quarter components skews the risks to
our Q4 trimmed mean estimate of 0.9%qtr up. The RBA is unlikely to relax its focus on price risks until

well into the new year.
This week, Australia also received two partial indicators for investment ahead of next Wednesday’s Q3
GDP report.
Construction activity was in line with Westpac’s expectation, up 0.9% in Q3 and 5.8% over the year.
The detail shows a clear rotation in activity: growth in infrastructure work moderating (1.3%yr) as major
public projects roll off; while momentum in residential (9.3%yr) and non-residential construction
(10.2%yr) pick up. Further, a strong pipeline of projects remains in place, ready to soak up any

additional capacity released by public infrastructure completions.
Private CAPEX subsequently surprised to the upside, up 12.2% in Q3, surpassing Westpac’s top-of-
the-market forecast of 10%. The headline result was driven by another very strong showing for data
centre-related investment, represented by a 184% surge in machinery and equipment spending in
information media and telecommunications. There are also signs of a nascent uptrend in investment

forming across other sectors, including utilities, financial and insurance services, and retail trade. At
16%yr, real (inflation-adjusted) capex plans for 2026-27 are the strongest since the mining investment
boom.
Ahead of next Wednesday’s release, our Q3 GDP preview will be published later today on Westpac IQ.
Offshore this week, the data released corroborated known themes. Ahead of Thanksgiving, US Q3
GDP growth was revised up from 3.4% annualised to 4.3% in the second estimate. Underlying the

result was stronger growth in personal consumption, revised up from 3.8% annualised to 4.2%, and a
broad-based upgrade for business investment. That said, monthly PCE data suggests consumer
momentum waned at quarter end, with real spending up just 0.1% in September and unchanged in
October despite an acceleration in nominal personal income growth (from 0.3% to 0.7%).
The Federal Reserve's Beige Book remains more downbeat than the hard data. "Eight of the twelve

Federal Reserve Districts reported growth in the slight to moderate range, while four Districts reported
no change. Consumer spending was little changed, as households remained price sensitive and were
increasingly selective in discretionary purchases." Given weak population growth and uncertainty over
momentum outside the tech sector, employers are " focused on utilizing existing staff more efficiently”,
“with many firms reporting stable head counts and hiring primarily to replace for attrition”. These

findings suggest that, should the pace of expansion in AI infrastructure plateau or turn, US GDP could
quickly return near trend.
The market’s mood continues to depend not only on the data to hand but also ongoing threats to
Middle East oil supply, Houthi rebels struck Saudi assets again this week with limited effect, and

### Page 3

Related articles
expectations for the AI buildout, which this week was dominated by news around NVIDIA. US trade
policy also remains a threat, the Trump administration this week announcing plans for a fresh round of
tariffs on China, the European Union and several other major trading partners to replace previous
levies struck down by the Supreme Court. China’s 20% tariff could double to 40% should trade
negotiations break down. India may also face a 25% tariff if it fails to reach a trade agreement with the

US. Uncertainty is likely to persist on each front well into 2027.

Document 358: Japan’s Economic Outlook for FY 2026–2028 (Nov. 2026)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Japan’s Economic Outlook for FY 2026–2028 (Nov. 2026).pdf

Date: 2026-11-24

### Page 1

< Real GDP growth forecast at 0.9% in FY 2026, 
1.3% in FY 2027, and 1.1% in FY 2028 > 
1. Real GDP growth in the July–September quarter of 2026 was −0.1% on an annualized basis, 
marking the first contraction in four quarters, mainly due to a decline in private consumption. 
2. Real GDP growth in the October–December quarter of 2026 and the January–March quarter of 
2027 is expected to remain in the low- to mid-0% range. This is due in part to weak private 
consumption, driven mainly by declining real purchasing power as inflation accelerates. 
Consequently, Japan’s economy is likely to remain sluggish in the second half of FY 2026. 
3. In FY 2027, lower inflation following the consumption tax rate cut on food and beverages is 
expected to improve households’ real purchasing power and lead to a recovery in private 
consumption. Meanwhile, growth in private non-residential investment is expected to accelerate as 
lower raw material prices improve corporate profits. 
4. Real GDP growth is forecast at 0.9% in FY 2026, 1.3% in FY 2027, and 1.1% in FY 2028. The 
consumption tax rate cut is estimated to lower real GDP by 0.1% in FY 2026 and raise it by 0.3% 
in FY 2027. 
5. Consumer price inflation (all items excluding fresh food) is expected to accelerate to the 3% range 
toward the end of FY 2026 as rising import prices feed through to a broad range of items, 
including food and daily necessities. However, it is projected to slow to the 1% range in FY 2027 
following the consumption tax rate cut on food and beverages. Overall, consumer price inflation 
(all items excluding fresh food) is forecast at 2.4% in FY 2026, 1.5% in FY 2027, and 2.0% in FY 
2028.

1 AI-related goods comprise the 103 product categories classified as “AI-Enabling-Products” by the WTO. AI-related goods accounted for 
17.1% of total exports in 2025.

### Page 3

AI-related goods have recorded pronounced
increases in both prices and volumes, likely
reflecting tighter supply and demand conditions
for semiconductors and electronic components as
global AI investment expands. Export prices of
AI-related goods rose sharply by 24.3% year-on-
year in the July–September quarter of 2026.
Together with the increase in export volumes,
this resulted in strong 38.0% year-on-year
growth in export value. Expanding AI-related

exports are substantially boosting total export value on both a volume and price basis.
These increases in export prices, led by AI-related goods, are also helping to mitigate the deterioration in the
terms of trade associated with heightened tensions in the Middle East. While higher prices for crude oil and
other commodities worsen the terms of trade through rising import prices, export prices have also increased
substantially. As a result, the deterioration in the terms of trade index—calculated by dividing export prices

by import prices—has been relatively limited.
Looking at past episodes of surging commodity prices, during the global commodity price surge around
2005–2008, import prices for crude oil and other commodities rose sharply while export price increases
failed to keep pace, leading to a deterioration in the terms of trade. From 2020 onward, the terms of trade
also deteriorated significantly due to rising commodity prices during the post-COVID-19 global economic

recovery, the surge in commodity prices following Russia’s invasion of Ukraine in 2022, and yen
depreciation. In both episodes, the cumulative deterioration in the terms of trade was at least 30%. By
comparison, the deterioration over the two quarters from the January–March quarter to the July–September
quarter of 2026 was 6.6%. A major difference from past episodes is that higher export prices, led by AI-
related goods, have partially offset the impact

of rising import prices caused by surging
crude oil prices.
However, import prices are still rising faster
than export prices, and the terms of trade
continue to deteriorate. If crude oil prices
remain elevated, income outflows abroad
could weigh on the domestic economy.
Meanwhile, if export prices continue to rise
as AI-related demand expands, this will help
mitigate further deterioration in the terms of
trade.

### Page 4

The wage hike rate in the 2027 Spring Labor Offensive (Shunto) is expected to slightly exceed
the previous year’s level
Real wages, calculated by deflating nominal wages using consumer prices (all items excluding imputed rent),
have maintained positive growth for nine consecutive months since January 2026. Nominal wage (total cash
earnings) growth accelerated from the low-2% range in 2025 to the 3% range after the start of 2026. In
addition, a substantial slowdown in inflation—partly due to government measures to ease the burden of

energy costs—has contributed to the increase in real wages.
Although real wages have recently begun to
recover, they had remained on a downward trend
for approximately four years from 2022, as
nominal wage growth failed to keep pace with
inflation. Compared with the pre-COVID-19
period in 2019, nominal wages in the July–
September quarter of 2026 were 12.6% higher,
whereas consumer prices (all items excluding
imputed rent) had risen by an even larger 16.8%

over the same period. Thus, despite the recovery
since the start of 2026, real wages remain 3.7%
below pre-COVID-19 levels, meaning nominal wage increases have not yet fully offset the erosion in
purchasing power caused by higher prices.
The environment surrounding the 2027 Spring Labor Offensive shows that labor market conditions remain
tight, with the active job openings-to-applicants ratio above 1 and the unemployment rate in the mid-2%
range. Corporate profits continue to reach new record highs despite the impact of heightened tensions in the

Middle East. Consumer price inflation had remained in the 1% range since the start of 2026 due to
government energy relief measures, but it rose to the 2% range in September for the first time in nine months
and is certain to accelerate.
In its basic framework for the 2027 Spring Labor Offensive announced on October 22, the Japanese Trade
Union Confederation (Rengo) set a wage hike guideline of “around 6%, with a minimum of 5% (including
the equivalent of regular pay increases),” raising its demands from the previous year’s “at least 5%.” It also

set a guideline of “¥20,000 and at least 6%” for unions at small and midsize companies, including an
additional amount to narrow wage disparities. In recent years, employers’ offers in the Spring Labor
Offensive have generally been slightly less than 1 percentage point below union demands. Since 2023, labor
unions’ wage demands have risen sharply due to high prices and labor shortages, and actual wage hike rates
have risen accordingly, keeping the gap between demands and offers relatively stable. Given this pattern, if

Rengo’s affiliated unions raise their wage demands in line with its policy, employers are also likely to raise
their offers, pushing up the overall wage hike rate.

Against this backdrop, the current outlook
forecasts a wage hike rate of 5.10% for the 2027
Spring Labor Offensive, remaining high in the
5% range for the fourth consecutive year and
rising slightly from 5.01% in the previous year
(on the Ministry of Health, Labour and Welfare

with the actual result of 5.18% in 2026). The
strong possibility that consumer price inflation
will accelerate from its current level of around

2% to the 3% range around the beginning of 2027—when wage negotiations get into full swing—should also
support wage increases.
Real wages have continued to grow by around 2% year-on-year, but growth is likely to slow gradually as
consumer price inflation accelerates, temporarily turning negative toward the end of FY 2026. However,
growth is expected to turn positive after the start of FY 2027 as the consumption tax rate cut on food and
beverages slows consumer price inflation. In FY 2028, once the effect of the consumption tax rate cut runs

its course, consumer price inflation will rise to around 2%. However, continued large wage hikes will sustain
nominal wage growth in the 3% range, keeping real wage growth at around 1%.
2. Real GDP growth forecast at 0.9% in FY 2026, 1.3% in FY 2027, and 1.1% in
FY 2028
Low growth expected to continue in the second half of FY 2026
The decline in private consumption—the main cause of negative growth in the July–September quarter of
2026—largely reflected the temporary effects of adverse weather. Even after these effects dissipate, however,

accelerating inflation is likely to hinder a recovery in consumption. Consumer price inflation returned to the
2% range in September 2026 for the first time in nine months, but is likely to rise to the 3% range toward the
end of FY 2026 as the pass-through of higher raw material prices to durable goods and daily necessities
becomes more widespread. Private consumption is therefore expected to remain weak in the second half of
FY 2026 as higher inflation erodes real purchasing power.

At present, growth is expected to turn positive in the October–December quarter of 2026, but with private
consumption struggling to grow, it is projected to reach only 0.6% on an annualized basis. In the January–
March quarter of 2027, private consumption is likely to decline quarter-on-quarter as inflation rises to the 3%
range and consumers hold off on purchases ahead of the consumption tax rate cut on food and beverages.
Real GDP growth is therefore likely to remain close to zero, at 0.2% on an annualized basis. Overall, Japan’s

economy is expected to remain sluggish in the second half of FY 2026, mainly because high prices depress
private consumption.

Private non-residential investment continues to recover on an underlying basis, supported by high corporate
profits, but the pace of recovery is likely to remain modest for the time being due to high construction
material prices and delays in construction progress caused by labor shortages. Although global AI-related
demand will support exports, overall growth is expected to remain modest given the persistent drag from the
situation in the Middle East and subdued shipments to China remaining amid weakness in its domestic

demand. As a result, exports are unlikely to drive economic growth.
In FY 2027, lower inflation following the consumption tax rate cut is expected to improve households’ real
purchasing power and lead to a recovery in private consumption. Meanwhile, growth in private non-
residential investment is expected to accelerate as lower raw material prices improve corporate profits.
Growth in the April–June quarter of 2027 is likely to be strong at 2.8% on an annualized basis, mainly driven

by a surge in private consumption as improved real purchasing power reinforces the rebound from pre-tax-
cut spending restraint. Thereafter, growth is expected to continue in the low-1% range on an annualized
basis—above the potential growth rate—led by domestic private demand such as private consumption and
non-residential investment. In FY 2028, although the effect of the consumption tax rate cut will fade, the
continued recovery in private consumption and ongoing increases in capital investment are likely to sustain

growth of around 1% on an annualized basis.
Real GDP growth is forecast at 0.9% in FY
2026, 1.3% in FY 2027, and 1.1% in FY 2028.
The institute estimates that consumers holding
off on purchases ahead of the consumption tax
rate cut on food and beverages will lower real
GDP in FY 2026 by 0.1%, while the subsequent
rebound and improved real purchasing power
from lower inflation will raise real GDP in FY
2027 by 0.3%.
Households’ real disposable income remains below its pre-COVID-19 level

Sluggish household consumption since the start of FY 2026 partly reflects the reclassification of spending as
government consumption following the introduction of free high school tuition and elementary school meals,
as well as adverse weather, including heavy and prolonged rain. Over a longer horizon, however, weak
growth in real disposable income is the primary cause. Households’ real disposable income (seasonally
adjusted) has recently recovered but remains slightly below its pre-COVID-19 level (the 2019 average).

Private consumption is forecast to grow by 0.0% in FY 2026, 1.4% in FY 2027, and 0.9% in FY 2028 year-

Price outlook
The rate of increase in consumer prices (all items excluding fresh food, hereafter core CPI) fell to 1.9% year-
on-year in January 2026, dropping below 2% for the first time in three years and ten months (since March
2022). It subsequently remained in the 1% range before rising to 2.1% year-on-year in September, returning
to the 2% range for the first time in nine months.

The rate of increase in food prices (excluding fresh food) continued to slow after peaking at 8.3% year-on-
year in July 2025, reaching 2.7% in August 2026 before accelerating for the first time in 14 months to 2.9%
in September. According to Teikoku Databank’s “Survey of Price Trends at 195 Major Food Companies”
(released October 30), the number of food and beverage items subject to price increases in 2026 (January–
December) is 20,327, slightly below the 20,580 items recorded in the same period of the previous year. Amid

the surge in raw material prices associated with the deteriorating situation in the Middle East, the number of
items slated for price increases over the next three months has risen year-on-year since June 2026, although
the pace has slowed somewhat. The rate of increase in consumer food prices (excluding fresh food) is
expected to accelerate, though it should remain more moderate than in the summer of 2025, partly because
rice prices are expected to continue declining for the time being.

Meanwhile, energy prices have been restrained by the government’s relief measures, but support for
electricity and gas bills temporarily ended with September 2026 usage (reflected in October consumer
prices). Although support will resume for usage starting in December 2026, electricity and gas bills this
winter are expected to be substantially higher than in the previous year. This is because subsidy amounts will
be slightly lower than last year, and the impact of surging crude oil prices since the spring of 2026 will be

reflected in fuel cost adjustment rates with a lag. Additionally, to curb surging gasoline prices, the
government used subsidies to hold the nationwide average retail price of regular gasoline at approximately
¥170 per liter from March through November 2026, but it has announced plans to gradually reduce subsidies
from December onward and raise the benchmark price by ¥5 each month. Consequently, energy price
inflation is expected to accelerate sharply from −0.6% year-on-year in September 2026, reaching double

digits year-on-year after the start of 2027 and contributing more than 1 percentage point to core CPI
inflation.
This outlook assumes that support for electricity and gas bills will continue from the summer of 2027
onward, with subsidies gradually reduced. For gasoline subsidies, the benchmark price is assumed to be
raised in stages to ¥185 per liter in February 2027 and then held at that level.
Core CPI inflation is expected to continue trending upward during FY 2026 and reach the 3% range toward

the end of FY 2026 as rising import prices, led by crude oil, feed through to a broad range of items, including
energy, food, durable goods, and daily necessities. A mechanical estimate of the impact of the consumption
tax rate cut for food and beverages from April 2027 onward indicates that core CPI inflation will be reduced

ANALYST: Tajinder Dhillon, CFA October 30, 2026 
tajinder.dhillon@lseg.com; 
THIS WEEK IN EARNINGS 
AGGREGATE ESTIMATES AND REVISIONS 
 26Q3 Y/Y earnings are expected to be 31.6%. Excluding the energy sector, the Y/Y earnings estimate is 26.4%. 
 Of the 299 companies in the S&P 500 that have reported earnings to date for 26Q3, 81.6% have reported earnings 
above analyst estimates. This compares to a long-term average of 67.7% and prior four quarter average of 81.5%. 
 26Q3 Y/Y revenue is expected to be 13.6%. Excluding the energy sector, the growth estimate is 12.2%. 
 69.9% of companies have reported 26Q3 revenue above analyst expectations. This compares to a long-term 
average of 63% and an average over the past four quarters of 76.8%. 
 For 26Q3, there have been 40 negative EPS preannouncements issued by S&P 500 corporations compared to 66 
positive EPS preannouncements. By dividing 40 by 66 the N/P ratio is 0.6 for the S&P 500 Index.  
 The forward four-quarter (26Q4– 27Q3) P/E ratio for the S&P 500 is 19.0. 
 During the week of Nov. 2, 144 S&P 500 companies are expected to report quarterly earnings. 
SECTION A: EARNINGS OUTLOOK 
Q3 2026: EARNINGS REVISIONS 
EXHIBIT 1A.  S&P 500: Q3 2026 SHARE-WEIGHTED EARNINGS ($B)

### Page 2

THIS WEEK IN EARNINGS 
This Week in Earnings provides analysis and commentary on aggregate earnings estimate revisions, growth rates and valuations.  2 
There has been an increase in the share-weighted earnings for the S&P 500 since the start of the quarter (to $778.4B 
from $760.7B). Three of the eleven sectors have experienced downward revisions to estimates.

Since Aug. 1, the communication services (-20.5%) and materials (-7%) sectors have recorded the highest percentage 
decreases in earnings, while the energy (33%) sector has recorded the highest percentage increase in earnings. 
Overall, share-weighted earnings for the S&P 500 have increased by 2.3% since the start of the quarter.

Since Aug. 1, the communication services (-14.7B) and materials (-1.2B) sectors have recorded the highest dollar-level 
decreases in earnings, while the energy (15.7B) sector has recorded the highest dollar-level increase in earnings. 
Overall, expected share-weighted earnings for the S&P 500 have increased by 17.7B since the start of the quarter.

299 companies in the S&P 500 Index have reported earnings for Q3 2026. Of these companies, 81.6% reported 
earnings above analyst expectations and 15.7% reported earnings below analyst expectations. In a typical quarter 
(since 1994), 67% of companies beat estimates and 20% miss estimates. Over the past four quarters, 82% of 
companies beat the estimates and 15% missed estimates. 
In aggregate, companies are reporting earnings that are 4.5% above estimates, which compares to a long-term (since 
1994) average surprise factor of 4.4% and the average surprise factor over the prior four quarters of 7.7%.
